Here is the rebuilt, self-contained single-file index.html for ASTRAL 3D. It combines Stoic's calm editorial rhythm, ChatGPT/Claude-grade upright typographic clarity, a center + quick-action navigation bar, full EN / HI / BN / Hinglish language switching, local localStorage journaling, live 1080×1920 PNG export, and five interactive Three.js celestial canvases.

function renderRashiSVG(id, size = 34, stroke = 'currentColor') {
  return `<svg width="${size}" height="${size}" viewBox="0 0 72 72" fill="none" stroke="${stroke}" stroke-width="1.6" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round">${CONSTELL_SVG[id] || ''}</svg>`;
}

  kumbha: [[-1.2, 0.3, 0.1], [-0.5, -0.2, 0.2], [0.2, 0.4, -0.1], [0.9, -0.2, 0.1], [1.3, 0.3, 0]],
  meena: [[-0.9, 0.6, 0.1], [-0.4, -0.1, 0.2], [0.3, -0.1, 0.1], [0.9, 0.6, -0.1], [-0.2, -0.7, 0]]
};

let activeRashi = RASHIS[11]; // Default Meena
let chemA = RASHIS[3];        // Karkata
let chemB = RASHIS[8];        // Dhanu
let dossierTab = 'light';
let currentLang = 'en';
let storyTheme = 'obsidian';
let anonymousMode = false;
let reduceMotion = false;
let onboarded = false;

/* ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   THREE.JS SCENE MANAGERS
═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */
const scenes = {};

function makeStarField(count, spread, colorHex, opacity = 0.55) {
  const geo = new THREE.BufferGeometry();
  const pos = new Float32Array(count * 3);
  for (let i = 0; i < count * 3; i++) {
    pos[i] = (Math.random() - 0.5) * spread;
  }
  geo.setAttribute('position', new THREE.BufferAttribute(pos, 3));
  const mat = new THREE.PointsMaterial({
    color: colorHex,
    size: 0.045,
    transparent: true,
    opacity
  });
  return new THREE.Points(geo, mat);
}

function makeFacetedCrystal(radius, colorHex) {
  const geo = new THREE.IcosahedronGeometry(radius, 1);
  const pos = geo.attributes.position;
  for (let i = 0; i < pos.count; i++) {
    const jitter = 0.88 + ((i * 37) % 10) * 0.022;
    pos.setXYZ(i, pos.getX(i) * jitter, pos.getY(i) * jitter, pos.getZ(i) * jitter);
  }
  pos.needsUpdate = true;
  geo.computeVertexNormals();

  const mat = new THREE.MeshPhysicalMaterial({
    color: new THREE.Color(colorHex),
    metalness: 0.06,
    roughness: 0.14,
    transmission: 0.58,
    thickness: 1.0,
    ior: 1.65,
    iridescence: 0.9,
    iridescenceIOR: 1.45,
    clearcoat: 1.0,
    clearcoatRoughness: 0.08,
    transparent: true,
    opacity: 0.92
  });

  const group = new THREE.Group();
  const mesh = new THREE.Mesh(geo, mat);
  const wire = new THREE.Mesh(
    geo.clone(),
    new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({
      color: new THREE.Color(colorHex),
      wireframe: true,
      transparent: true,
      opacity: 0.18
    })
  );
  wire.scale.setScalar(0.84);
  group.add(mesh, wire);
  return { group, mat };
}

function makeOrbitRing(radius, colorHex, opacity = 0.25) {
  const geo = new THREE.TorusGeometry(radius, 0.006, 8, 128);
  const mat = new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ color: colorHex, transparent: true, opacity });
  return new THREE.Mesh(geo, mat);
}

/* 1. Onboarding 3D Opalescent Crystal */
function initOnboarding3D() {
  const canvas = document.getElementById('canvas-onboarding');
  const wrap = canvas.parentElement;
  const renderer = new THREE.WebGLRenderer({ canvas, antialias: true, alpha: true });
  renderer.setPixelRatio(Math.min(window.devicePixelRatio, 2));
  renderer.setSize(wrap.clientWidth, wrap.clientHeight, false);

  const scene = new THREE.Scene();
  const camera = new THREE.PerspectiveCamera(48, wrap.clientWidth / wrap.clientHeight, 0.1, 100);
  camera.position.set(0, 0, 4.4);

  scene.add(makeStarField(420, 18, 0xC8B6E2, 0.55));
  const { group: crystal } = makeFacetedCrystal(0.92, '#C8B6E2');
  scene.add(crystal);

  const ring1 = makeOrbitRing(1.38, 0xC8B6E2, 0.24);
  ring1.rotation.x = Math.PI / 2.6;
  const ring2 = makeOrbitRing(1.66, 0xE2C285, 0.18);
  ring2.rotation.x = Math.PI / 3.2;
  ring2.rotation.y = Math.PI / 5;
  scene.add(ring1, ring2);

  scene.add(new THREE.AmbientLight(0xffffff, 0.85));
  const l1 = new THREE.PointLight(0xC8B6E2, 4, 12); l1.position.set(2.5, 2, 3);
  const l2 = new THREE.PointLight(0xE2C285, 3.2, 12); l2.position.set(-2.5, -1.5, 2.5);
  scene.add(l1, l2);

  let targetX = 0, targetY = 0;
  wrap.addEventListener('pointermove', (e) => {
    const r = wrap.getBoundingClientRect();
    targetX = ((e.clientX - r.left) / r.width - 0.5) * 0.9;
    targetY = -((e.clientY - r.top) / r.height - 0.5) * 0.7;
  });

  scenes.onboarding = { renderer, scene, camera, crystal, ring1, ring2, targetX: () => targetX, targetY: () => targetY };
}

/* 2. Today 3D Constellation + Lunar Phase Orb */
function initToday3D() {
  const canvas = document.getElementById('canvas-today');
  const wrap = canvas.parentElement;
  const renderer = new THREE.WebGLRenderer({ canvas, antialias: true, alpha: true });
  renderer.setPixelRatio(Math.min(window.devicePixelRatio, 2));
  renderer.setSize(wrap.clientWidth, wrap.clientHeight, false);

  const scene = new THREE.Scene();
  const camera = new THREE.PerspectiveCamera(48, wrap.clientWidth / wrap.clientHeight, 0.1, 100);
  camera.position.set(0, 0, 4.3);

  scene.add(makeStarField(360, 16, 0x9498A6, 0.45));

  // Lunar Phase Orb (lit from right side for Waxing Gibbous 75%)
  const moonGeo = new THREE.SphereGeometry(0.42, 32, 32);
  const moonMat = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({
    color: 0xE6E7EE,
    roughness: 0.75,
    metalness: 0.05
  });
  const moon = new THREE.Mesh(moonGeo, moonMat);
  moon.position.set(1.15, 0.42, -0.2);
  scene.add(moon);

  const moonLight = new THREE.DirectionalLight(0xFFF6E4, 2.6);
  moonLight.position.set(3.2, 0.9, 1.4);
  scene.add(moonLight);
  scene.add(new THREE.AmbientLight(0x303442, 0.55));

  // Constellation group (rebuilt per activeRashi)
  const constGroup = new THREE.Group();
  constGroup.position.set(-0.35, -0.05, 0);
  scene.add(constGroup);

  let dragRotY = 0, dragRotX = 0, isDown = false, lastX = 0, lastY = 0;
  wrap.addEventListener('pointerdown', (e) => { isDown = true; lastX = e.clientX; lastY = e.clientY; });
  window.addEventListener('pointerup', () => { isDown = false; });
  wrap.addEventListener('pointermove', (e) => {
    if (!isDown) return;
    dragRotY += (e.clientX - lastX) * 0.01;
    dragRotX += (e.clientY - lastY) * 0.01;
    lastX = e.clientX; lastY = e.clientY;
  });

  scenes.today = {
    renderer, scene, camera, constGroup, moon,
    getDrag: () => ({ x: dragRotX, y: dragRotY })
  };
  rebuildTodayConstellation();
}

function rebuildTodayConstellation() {
  if (!scenes.today) return;
  const { constGroup } = scenes.today;
  while (constGroup.children.length) constGroup.remove(constGroup.children[0]);

  const pts = (CONSTELL_3D[activeRashi.id] || CONSTELL_3D.meena).map(p => new THREE.Vector3(p[0], p[1], p[2]));
  const col = new THREE.Color(activeRashi.color);

  const lineGeo = new THREE.BufferGeometry().setFromPoints(pts);
  const lineMat = new THREE.LineBasicMaterial({ color: col, transparent: true, opacity: 0.65 });
  constGroup.add(new THREE.Line(lineGeo, lineMat));

  pts.forEach((pt, idx) => {
    const node = new THREE.Mesh(
      new THREE.SphereGeometry(idx === 0 ? 0.065 : 0.048, 16, 16),
      new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ color: idx === 0 ? 0xF4F4F6 : col })
    );
    node.position.copy(pt);
    constGroup.add(node);
  });
}

/* 3. Dossier 3D 12-Node Zodiac Ring (lerps to selected sign) */
function initDossier3D() {
  const canvas = document.getElementById('canvas-dossier');
  const wrap = canvas.parentElement;
  const renderer = new THREE.WebGLRenderer({ canvas, antialias: true, alpha: true });
  renderer.setPixelRatio(Math.min(window.devicePixelRatio, 2));
  renderer.setSize(wrap.clientWidth, wrap.clientHeight, false);

  const scene = new THREE.Scene();
  const camera = new THREE.PerspectiveCamera(45, wrap.clientWidth / wrap.clientHeight, 0.1, 100);
  camera.position.set(0, 1.35, 4.1);
  camera.lookAt(0, 0, 0);

  scene.add(makeStarField(260, 14, 0x9498A6, 0.4));

  const wheel = new THREE.Group();
  const ring = makeOrbitRing(1.55, 0x636775, 0.32);
  ring.rotation.x = Math.PI / 2;
  wheel.add(ring);

  const nodes = [];
  RASHIS.forEach((r, i) => {
    const angle = (i / 12) * Math.PI * 2;
    const mesh = new THREE.Mesh(
      new THREE.OctahedronGeometry(0.11, 0),
      new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({
        color: new THREE.Color(r.color),
        emissive: new THREE.Color(r.color),
        emissiveIntensity: 0.25,
        roughness: 0.25,
        metalness: 0.2
      })
    );
    mesh.position.set(Math.sin(angle) * 1.55, 0, Math.cos(angle) * 1.55);
    wheel.add(mesh);
    nodes.push({ mesh, id: r.id, angle });
  });

  scene.add(wheel);
  scene.add(new THREE.AmbientLight(0xffffff, 0.9));
  const frontLight = new THREE.PointLight(0xffffff, 2.5, 10);
  frontLight.position.set(0, 1.5, 3);
  scene.add(frontLight);

  scenes.dossier = { renderer, scene, camera, wheel, nodes, targetAngle: 0 };
  updateDossier3DTarget();
}

function updateDossier3DTarget() {
  if (!scenes.dossier) return;
  const idx = RASHIS.findIndex(r => r.id === activeRashi.id);
  scenes.dossier.targetAngle = -(idx / 12) * Math.PI * 2;
  scenes.dossier.nodes.forEach(n => {
    const active = n.id === activeRashi.id;
    n.mesh.scale.setScalar(active ? 1.55 : 0.9);
    n.mesh.material.emissiveIntensity = active ? 0.9 : 0.18;
  });
}

/* 4. Alchemy (Chemistry) 3D Dual Orbits */
function initChemistry3D() {
  const canvas = document.getElementById('canvas-chemistry');
  const wrap = canvas.parentElement;
  const renderer = new THREE.WebGLRenderer({ canvas, antialias: true, alpha: true });
  renderer.setPixelRatio(Math.min(window.devicePixelRatio, 2));
  renderer.setSize(wrap.clientWidth, wrap.clientHeight, false);

  const scene = new THREE.Scene();
  const camera = new THREE.PerspectiveCamera(46, wrap.clientWidth / wrap.clientHeight, 0.1, 100);
  camera.position.set(0, 0.3, 4.2);

  scene.add(makeStarField(260, 14, 0x9498A6, 0.4));

  const cryA = makeFacetedCrystal(0.42, chemA.color);
  const cryB = makeFacetedCrystal(0.42, chemB.color);
  scene.add(cryA.group, cryB.group);

  const pathA = makeOrbitRing(1.1, 0x9498A6, 0.22);
  pathA.rotation.x = Math.PI / 2.2;
  pathA.rotation.y = 0.25;
  const pathB = makeOrbitRing(1.1, 0x9498A6, 0.22);
  pathB.rotation.x = Math.PI / 2.2;
  pathB.rotation.y = -0.25;
  scene.add(pathA, pathB);

  const beamGeo = new THREE.BufferGeometry().setFromPoints([new THREE.Vector3(-1, 0, 0), new THREE.Vector3(1, 0, 0)]);
  const beam = new THREE.Line(beamGeo, new THREE.LineBasicMaterial({ color: 0xC8B6E2, transparent: true, opacity: 0.45 }));
  scene.add(beam);

  scene.add(new THREE.AmbientLight(0xffffff, 0.9));
  const pl = new THREE.PointLight(0xffffff, 2.5, 10);
  pl.position.set(0, 2, 3);
  scene.add(pl);

  scenes.chemistry = { renderer, scene, camera, cryA, cryB, beam };
}

function updateChemistry3DColors() {
  if (!scenes.chemistry) return;
  scenes.chemistry.cryA.mat.color.set(chemA.color);
  scenes.chemistry.cryB.mat.color.set(chemB.color);
}

/* 5. Studio 3D Card Background (preserveDrawingBuffer for crisp PNG export) */
function initStudio3D() {
  const canvas = document.getElementById('canvas-studio');
  const wrap = canvas.parentElement;
  const renderer = new THREE.WebGLRenderer({
    canvas,
    antialias: true,
    alpha: true,
    preserveDrawingBuffer: true
  });
  renderer.setPixelRatio(Math.min(window.devicePixelRatio, 2));
  renderer.setSize(wrap.clientWidth, wrap.clientHeight, false);

  const scene = new THREE.Scene();
  const camera = new THREE.PerspectiveCamera(48, wrap.clientWidth / wrap.clientHeight, 0.1, 100);
  camera.position.set(0, 0, 4.5);

  const stars = makeStarField(320, 14, 0xC8B6E2, 0.5);
  const { group: crystal, mat } = makeFacetedCrystal(0.68, activeRashi.color);
  crystal.position.set(0, 0.5, 0);
  const ring = makeOrbitRing(1.25, 0xE2C285, 0.22);
  ring.position.set(0, 0.5, 0);
  ring.rotation.x = Math.PI / 2.8;

  scene.add(stars, crystal, ring);
  scene.add(new THREE.AmbientLight(0xffffff, 0.9));
  const l1 = new THREE.PointLight(0xffffff, 2.8, 12); l1.position.set(2, 2, 3);
  scene.add(l1);

  scenes.studio = { renderer, scene, camera, crystal, mat, ring, stars };
}

/* Unified Animation Loop */
let clockT = 0;
function animateAll() {
  requestAnimationFrame(animateAll);
  if (!reduceMotion) clockT += 0.012;

  if (activeScreen === 'onboarding' && scenes.onboarding) {
    const s = scenes.onboarding;
    if (!reduceMotion) {
      s.crystal.rotation.y += (clockT * 0.35 + s.targetX() - s.crystal.rotation.y) * 0.06;
      s.crystal.rotation.x += (Math.sin(clockT * 0.4) * 0.18 + s.targetY() - s.crystal.rotation.x) * 0.06;
      s.ring1.rotation.z = clockT * 0.15;
      s.ring2.rotation.z = -clockT * 0.11;
    }
    s.renderer.render(s.scene, s.camera);
  }

  if (activeScreen === 'today' && scenes.today) {
    const s = scenes.today;
    const d = s.getDrag();
    if (!reduceMotion) {
      s.constGroup.rotation.y = Math.sin(clockT * 0.4) * 0.18 + d.y;
      s.constGroup.rotation.x = Math.cos(clockT * 0.3) * 0.08 + d.x;
    }
    s.renderer.render(s.scene, s.camera);
  }

  if (activeScreen === 'dossier' && scenes.dossier) {
    const s = scenes.dossier;
    s.wheel.rotation.y += (s.targetAngle - s.wheel.rotation.y) * (reduceMotion ? 1 : 0.09);
    s.renderer.render(s.scene, s.camera);
  }

  if (activeScreen === 'chemistry' && scenes.chemistry) {
    const s = scenes.chemistry;
    const a = reduceMotion ? 0.3 : clockT * 0.55;
    s.cryA.group.position.set(Math.cos(a) * 1.05, Math.sin(a * 0.7) * 0.22, Math.sin(a) * 0.55);
    s.cryB.group.position.set(Math.cos(a + Math.PI) * 1.05, -Math.sin(a * 0.7) * 0.22, Math.sin(a + Math.PI) * 0.55);
    s.cryA.group.rotation.y = clockT * 0.5;
    s.cryB.group.rotation.y = -clockT * 0.5;
    const posAttr = s.beam.geometry.attributes.position;
    posAttr.setXYZ(0, s.cryA.group.position.x, s.cryA.group.position.y, s.cryA.group.position.z);
    posAttr.setXYZ(1, s.cryB.group.position.x, s.cryB.group.position.y, s.cryB.group.position.z);
    posAttr.needsUpdate = true;
    s.renderer.render(s.scene, s.camera);
  }

  if (activeScreen === 'studio' && scenes.studio) {
    const s = scenes.studio;
    if (!reduceMotion) {
      s.crystal.rotation.y = clockT * 0.32;
      s.crystal.rotation.x = Math.sin(clockT * 0.4) * 0.14;
      s.ring.rotation.z = clockT * 0.12;
    }
    s.renderer.render(s.scene, s.camera);
  }
}

function handleResize() {
  Object.values(scenes).forEach(s => {
    if (!s || !s.renderer) return;
    const canvas = s.renderer.domElement;
    const wrap = canvas.parentElement;
    if (!wrap || wrap.clientWidth === 0 || wrap.clientHeight === 0) return;
    s.camera.aspect = wrap.clientWidth / wrap.clientHeight;
    s.camera.updateProjectionMatrix();
    s.renderer.setSize(wrap.clientWidth, wrap.clientHeight, false);
  });
}

/* ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   UI RENDERING & INTERACTIONS
═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */
function buildOnboardingGrid() {
  const grid = document.getElementById('onb-grid');
  grid.innerHTML = '';
  RASHIS.forEach(r => {
    const tile = document.createElement('button');
    tile.className = 'rashi-tile';
    tile.onclick = () => selectInitialRashi(r);
    tile.innerHTML = `
      <div style="color:${r.color};">${renderRashiSVG(r.id, 34, r.color)}</div>
      <div class="r-name">${r.vedic}</div>
      <div class="r-meta">${r.western} · <span style="font-family:var(--ff-hi)">${r.hi}</span></div>
    `;
    grid.appendChild(tile);
  });
}

function buildWeekStrip() {
  const strip = document.getElementById('week-strip');
  const today = new Date();
  const dayNames = ['Su', 'Mo', 'Tu', 'We', 'Th', 'Fr', 'Sa'];
  // Build Mon-Sun around today
  const mondayOffset = (today.getDay() + 6) % 7;
  const start = new Date(today);
  start.setDate(today.getDate() - mondayOffset);

  strip.innerHTML = '';
  for (let i = 0; i < 7; i++) {
    const d = new Date(start);
    d.setDate(start.getDate() + i);
    const isToday = d.toDateString() === today.toDateString();
    const el = document.createElement('div');
    el.className = 'day-pill' + (isToday ? ' today' : '');
    el.innerHTML = `
      <span class="d-wd">${dayNames[d.getDay()]}</span>
      <span class="d-num">${d.getDate()}</span>
    `;
    strip.appendChild(el);
  }
}

function selectInitialRashi(r) {
  activeRashi = r;
  chemA = r;
  onboarded = true;
  document.getElementById('bottom-nav').style.display = 'block';
  navTo('today');
  setTimeout(() => {
    if (!scenes.today) initToday3D();
    if (!scenes.dossier) initDossier3D();
    if (!scenes.chemistry) initChemistry3D();
    if (!scenes.studio) initStudio3D();
    handleResize();
    updateAllViews();
  }, 60);
}

window.navTo = function(screenId) {
  activeScreen = screenId;
  document.querySelectorAll('.screen').forEach(s => s.classList.remove('active'));
  document.getElementById('s-' + screenId).classList.add('active');

  document.querySelectorAll('.nav-item').forEach(btn => {
    btn.classList.toggle('active', btn.dataset.screen === screenId);
  });

  setTimeout(() => {
    if (screenId === 'today' && !scenes.today) initToday3D();
    if (screenId === 'dossier' && !scenes.dossier) initDossier3D();
    if (screenId === 'chemistry' && !scenes.chemistry) initChemistry3D();
    if (screenId === 'studio' && !scenes.studio) initStudio3D();
    handleResize();
  }, 40);
};

function updateAllViews() {
  updateTodayView();
  updateDossierView();
  updateChemistryView();
  updateStudioView();
}

function updateTodayView() {
  const r = activeRashi;
  const copy = LANG_COPY[currentLang];
  const hr = new Date().getHours();
  const gIdx = hr < 12 ? 0 : hr < 17 ? 1 : 2;

  document.getElementById('td-greeting').textContent = copy.greetings[gIdx];
  document.getElementById('td-pill-name').textContent = r.vedic;
  document.getElementById('td-hero-sub').textContent = copy.newDay;
  document.getElementById('td-headline').textContent = r.todayMood;
  document.getElementById('td-reflect-btn').textContent = copy.reflect;
  document.getElementById('td-ref-title').textContent = `${copy.refLabel} · ${r.vedic}`;
  document.getElementById('td-ref-body').textContent = r.todayReflection;
  document.getElementById('td-prompt-lbl').textContent = copy.promptLabel;
  document.getElementById('td-prompt-text').textContent = r.journalPrompt;
  document.getElementById('journal-prompt-echo').textContent = r.journalPrompt;

  document.getElementById('td-meters-title').textContent = copy.metersTitle;
  document.getElementById('lbl-intuition').textContent = copy.intuition;
  document.getElementById('lbl-clarity').textContent = copy.clarity;
  document.getElementById('lbl-heart').textContent = copy.heart;

  const [m1, m2, m3] = r.meters;
  document.getElementById('val-intuition').textContent = `${m1}%`;
  document.getElementById('bar-intuition').style.width = `${m1}%`;
  document.getElementById('val-clarity').textContent = `${m2}%`;
  document.getElementById('bar-clarity').style.width = `${m2}%`;
  document.getElementById('val-heart').textContent = `${m3}%`;
  document.getElementById('bar-heart').style.width = `${m3}%`;

  document.getElementById('td-chips').innerHTML = `
    <span class="meta-chip">Graha: <strong>${r.graha} (${r.grahaEn})</strong></span>
    <span class="meta-chip">Element: <strong>${r.elem}</strong></span>
    <span class="meta-chip">Tithi: <strong>Trayodashi</strong></span>
  `;

  rebuildTodayConstellation();
}

function renderDossierTab() {
  const r = activeRashi;
  const box = document.getElementById('dos-content');
  if (dossierTab === 'graha') {
    box.innerHTML = `
      <div class="stoic-card" style="margin-bottom:10px;">
        <div class="label-meta" style="margin-bottom:4px;">Ruling planet</div>
        <div class="h-display" style="font-size:24px;color:${r.color};">${r.graha}</div>
        <div class="h-sub" style="font-size:14px;">${r.grahaEn}</div>
      </div>
      <div class="planet-kv"><span class="label-human">Element</span><strong style="font-size:14px;">${r.elem}</strong></div>
      <div class="planet-kv"><span class="label-human">Western equivalent</span><strong style="font-size:14px;">${r.western}</strong></div>
      <div class="planet-kv"><span class="label-human">Hindi / Bengali</span><strong style="font-size:14px;">${r.hi} · ${r.bn}</strong></div>
    `;
    return;
  }

  const accentMap = { light: '#A7C7E7', shadow: '#C8B6E2', love: '#E2C285' };
  const barColor = accentMap[dossierTab];
  box.innerHTML = r[dossierTab].map(line => `
    <div class="trait-card">
      <div class="trait-accent" style="background:${barColor};"></div>
      <p class="trait-text">${line}</p>
    </div>
  `).join('');
}

/* Chemistry (Alchemy) */
function computeAlchemy(a, b) {
  const sameElem = a.elem === b.elem;
  const pair = [a.elem, b.elem].sort().join('-');
  const seed = (RASHIS.indexOf(a) * 7 + RASHIS.indexOf(b) * 13) % 18;

  let pull = 76 + (seed % 19);
  let friction = 44 + ((seed * 3) % 28);
  let depth = 82 + ((seed * 5) % 15);

  const labelA = anonymousMode ? 'Sign A' : a.western;
  const labelB = anonymousMode ? 'Sign B' : b.western;

  let pullText = `${labelA} and ${labelB} meet across ${a.elem.toLowerCase()} and ${b.elem.toLowerCase()} temperament — creating quiet fascination where each carries what the other forgot to pack.`;
  let frictionText = `${labelA} processes pressure through ${a.elem.toLowerCase()}, while ${labelB} responds through ${b.elem.toLowerCase()}. Most tension here is not lack of care; it is two different dialects asking for reassurance.`;
  let ruleText = `This bond steadies when ${labelA} stops assuming silence means distance, and ${labelB} remembers that consistency is just as compelling as excitement.`;

  if (sameElem) {
    pull = 84 + (seed % 10);
    friction = 36 + (seed % 16);
    pullText = `Two ${a.elem.toLowerCase()} skies recognize each other instantly. ${labelA} and ${labelB} share an unspoken rhythm, skipping surface small talk altogether.`;
  } else if (pair === 'Fire-Water') {
    pullText = `Water meets fire here — ${labelA} brings the harbor, ${labelB} brings the horizon. Together they reach places neither explores alone, provided the harbor trusts the ship to return.`;
    frictionText = `${labelB}'s restlessness can feel like distance to ${labelA}'s attachment, while ${labelA}'s depth can feel heavy to ${labelB}'s momentum.`;
  } else if (pair === 'Air-Earth') {
    pullText = `Idea meets form. ${labelA} and ${labelB} sharpen each other when curiosity stays anchored by patience.`;
    frictionText = `One wants a map before moving; the other prefers changing direction mid-sentence.`;
  }

  return { pull, friction, depth, pullText, frictionText, ruleText };
}

function updateChemistryView() {
  document.getElementById('ca-name').textContent = anonymousMode ? 'Sign A' : chemA.vedic;
  document.getElementById('ca-sub').textContent = anonymousMode ? chemA.elem : `${chemA.western} · ${chemA.elem}`;
  document.getElementById('cb-name').textContent = anonymousMode ? 'Sign B' : chemB.vedic;
  document.getElementById('cb-sub').textContent = anonymousMode ? chemB.elem : `${chemB.western} · ${chemB.elem}`;

  const res = computeAlchemy(chemA, chemB);
  document.getElementById('sc-pull').textContent = res.pull;
  document.getElementById('sc-friction').textContent = res.friction;
  document.getElementById('sc-depth').textContent = res.depth;

  document.getElementById('chem-pull-text').textContent = res.pullText;
  document.getElementById('chem-friction-text').textContent = res.frictionText;
  document.getElementById('chem-rule-text').textContent = res.ruleText;

  updateChemistry3DColors();
}

  anonymousMode = !anonymousMode;
  const el = document.getElementById('tog-anon');
  el.classList.toggle('on', anonymousMode);
  el.setAttribute('aria-checked', String(anonymousMode));
  updateChemistryView();
};

const STORY_THEMES = {
  obsidian: { bg: '#0B0C0E', fg: '#F4F4F6', crystal: '#C8B6E2' },
  paper: { bg: '#F7F6F2', fg: '#141518', crystal: '#8B7963' },

};

function updateStudioView() {
  const r = activeRashi;
  const th = STORY_THEMES[storyTheme];
  const frame = document.getElementById('story-frame');
  const overlay = document.getElementById('story-overlay');

  frame.style.background = th.bg;
  overlay.style.color = th.fg;

  const dateStr = new Date().toLocaleDateString('en-US', { month: 'short', day: 'numeric' });
  document.getElementById('st-date-tag').textContent = `astral · ${dateStr}`;
  document.getElementById('st-svg').innerHTML = renderRashiSVG(r.id, 48, th.fg);
  document.getElementById('st-name').textContent = r.vedic;
  document.getElementById('st-indic').textContent = `${r.western} · ${r.hi}`;
  document.getElementById('st-mood').textContent = r.todayMood;
  document.getElementById('st-reflection').textContent = r.todayReflection;

  if (scenes.studio) {
    scenes.studio.mat.color.set(storyTheme === 'paper' ? th.crystal : r.color);
    scenes.studio.renderer.render(scenes.studio.scene, scenes.studio.camera);
  }
}

window.exportStoryCard = function() {
  const W = 1080, H = 1920;
  const out = document.createElement('canvas');
  out.width = W;
  out.height = H;
  const ctx = out.getContext('2d');
  const th = STORY_THEMES[storyTheme];
  const r = activeRashi;

  ctx.fillStyle = th.bg;
  ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H);

  // Composite live Three.js 3D canvas cleanly into center
  const glCanvas = document.getElementById('canvas-studio');
  if (scenes.studio) {
    scenes.studio.renderer.render(scenes.studio.scene, scenes.studio.camera);
    ctx.drawImage(glCanvas, 0, 0, W, H);
  }

  ctx.fillStyle = th.fg;
  ctx.textAlign = 'center';

  // Top label
  ctx.globalAlpha = 0.7;
  ctx.font = '500 32px Inter, sans-serif';
  const dateStr = new Date().toLocaleDateString('en-US', { month: 'short', day: 'numeric' });
  ctx.fillText(`astral · ${dateStr}`, W / 2, 130);

  // Sign Name
  ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
  ctx.font = '600 84px "Plus Jakarta Sans", Inter, sans-serif';
  ctx.fillText(r.vedic, W / 2, 1080);

  ctx.globalAlpha = 0.75;
  ctx.font = '500 36px Inter, sans-serif';
  ctx.fillText(`${r.western} · ${r.hi}`, W / 2, 1145);

  // Wrapped mood & reflection text
  function wrapText(text, x, y, maxW, lineH, font, alpha = 1) {
    ctx.font = font;
    ctx.globalAlpha = alpha;
    const words = text.split(' ');
    let line = '', curY = y;
    for (const w of words) {
      const test = line + w + ' ';
      if (ctx.measureText(test).width > maxW && line) {
        ctx.fillText(line.trim(), x, curY);
        line = w + ' ';
        curY += lineH;
      } else {
        line = test;
      }
    }
    if (line.trim()) ctx.fillText(line.trim(), x, curY);
    return curY;
  }

  const nextY = wrapText(r.todayMood, W / 2, 1260, 820, 56, '500 40px Inter, sans-serif', 0.95);
  ctx.globalAlpha = 0.25;
  ctx.fillRect(W / 2 - 60, nextY + 46, 120, 2);
  wrapText(r.todayReflection, W / 2, nextY + 125, 840, 58, '400 38px Newsreader, Georgia, serif', 0.85);

  ctx.globalAlpha = 0.55;
  ctx.font = '500 30px Inter, sans-serif';
  ctx.fillText('Waxing Gibbous · 75%', W / 2, 1755);
  ctx.fillText('astral', W / 2, 1810);

  const link = document.createElement('a');
  link.download = `astral-${r.id}-story.png`;
  link.href = out.toDataURL('image/png');
  link.click();
  showToast('9:16 card saved');
};

function populateSwitcher(containerId, onPick) {
  const box = document.getElementById(containerId);
  box.innerHTML = '';
  RASHIS.forEach(r => {
    const btn = document.createElement('button');
    btn.className = 'sw-tile';
    btn.onclick = () => { onPick(r); closeAllSheets(); };
    btn.innerHTML = `
      <div>${renderRashiSVG(r.id, 28, r.color)}</div>
      <div style="font-size:13px;font-weight:600;color:var(--text-primary);">${r.vedic}</div>
      <div class="label-meta" style="font-size:11px;">${r.western}</div>
    `;
    box.appendChild(btn);
  });
}

/* Private Local Journal */
window.saveJournalEntry = function() {
  const input = document.getElementById('journal-input');
  const text = input.value.trim();
  if (!text) return;

  const entries = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(JOURNAL_KEY) || '[]');
  entries.unshift({
    text,
    rashi: activeRashi.vedic,
    date: new Date().toLocaleDateString('en-US', { month: 'short', day: 'numeric', hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit' })
  });
  localStorage.setItem(JOURNAL_KEY, JSON.stringify(entries.slice(0, 15)));
  input.value = '';
  renderJournalHistory();
  showToast('Saved locally');
};

function renderJournalHistory() {
  const box = document.getElementById('journal-history');
  const entries = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(JOURNAL_KEY) || '[]');
  if (!entries.length) {
    box.innerHTML = `<div class="label-meta" style="text-align:center;padding:8px 0;">Your private reflections will appear here.</div>`;
    return;
  }
  box.innerHTML = entries.slice(0, 4).map(e => `
    <div class="journal-entry">
      <div class="label-meta" style="margin-bottom:4px;">${e.rashi} · ${e.date}</div>
      <div style="font-size:14px;line-height:1.5;color:var(--text-primary);">${escapeHtml(e.text)}</div>
    </div>
  `).join('');
}

function escapeHtml(str) {
  return str.replace(/[&<>"']/g, m => ({ '&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;',"'":'&#39;' }[m]));
}

/* Birth Date Finder */
const bdDay = document.getElementById('bd-day');
const bdMonth = document.getElementById('bd-month');
const btnReveal = document.getElementById('btn-reveal-date');

function validateBirthInputs() {
  const d = +bdDay.value, m = +bdMonth.value;
  const ok = d >= 1 && d <= 31 && m >= 1 && m <= 12;
  btnReveal.disabled = !ok;
  btnReveal.style.opacity = ok ? '1' : '0.45';
}
bdDay.addEventListener('input', validateBirthInputs);
bdMonth.addEventListener('input', validateBirthInputs);

window.revealByDate = function() {
  const d = +bdDay.value, m = +bdMonth.value;
  let idx = 11;
  if ((m === 3 && d >= 21) || (m === 4 && d <= 19)) idx = 0;
  else if ((m === 4 && d >= 20) || (m === 5 && d <= 20)) idx = 1;
  else if ((m === 5 && d >= 21) || (m === 6 && d <= 20)) idx = 2;
  else if ((m === 6 && d >= 21) || (m === 7 && d <= 22)) idx = 3;
  else if ((m === 7 && d >= 23) || (m === 8 && d <= 22)) idx = 4;
  else if ((m === 8 && d >= 23) || (m === 9 && d <= 22)) idx = 5;
  else if ((m === 9 && d >= 23) || (m === 10 && d <= 22)) idx = 6;
  else if ((m === 10 && d >= 23) || (m === 11 && d <= 21)) idx = 7;
  else if ((m === 11 && d >= 22) || (m === 12 && d <= 21)) idx = 8;
  else if ((m === 12 && d >= 22) || (m === 1 && d <= 19)) idx = 9;
  else if ((m === 1 && d >= 20) || (m === 2 && d <= 18)) idx = 10;

  closeAllSheets();
  selectInitialRashi(RASHIS[idx]);
};

};

window.toggleReduceMotion = function() {
  const tog = document.getElementById('tog-motion');
  reduceMotion = tog.classList.toggle('on');
  tog.setAttribute('aria-checked', String(reduceMotion));
  if (reduceMotion) {
    if (!document.getElementById('reduce-motion-style')) {
      const s = document.createElement('style');
      s.id = 'reduce-motion-style';
      s.textContent = '*,*::before,*::after{transition-duration:0.01ms!important;animation-duration:0.01ms!important;}';
      document.head.appendChild(s);
    }
  } else {
    document.getElementById('reduce-motion-style')?.remove();
  }
};

function showToast(msg) {
  const t = document.getElementById('toast');
  t.textContent = msg;
  t.classList.add('show');
  setTimeout(() => t.classList.remove('show'), 2200);
}

Here is the full, self-contained **`index.html`** application uniting **UNSENT** with **Stoic's calm upright clarity** and **interactive Three.js 3D Zodiac Elements & Celestial Astrolabe visuals**.

### What is inside this complete build:
1. **Real Interactive 3D Zodiac Elements & Celestial Visuals (Three.js):**
   - **Today & Zodiac 3D Astrolabe:** Multi-ring golden astrolabe with glowing sun core, lunar phase orb, and 12 sign nodes.
   - **The Four Zodiac Elements in 3D:** Procedural, animated 3D sculptures for **Fire** (multi-layered flickering flame + rising spiral embers), **Earth** (faceted mountain peaks, moon sphere & floating mineral shards), **Air** (helical swirling wind ribbons + drifting cloud wisps), and **Water** (glossy undulating 3D ocean waves + crescent moon + rising mist droplets).
   - **Two-Sign Elemental Fusion:** Side-by-side 3D element sculptures interacting in orbit for conversation cards.
   - **3D Letter Release Ritual:** Choose **Paper Birds** (animated flapping 3D origami birds), **Embers**, or **Ash** (honors Reduce Motion).
2. **Deterministic Astronomical Engine (Zero LLM Math Hallucinations):**
   - Built-in Meeus-grade solar & lunar ephemeris computing tropical Sun longitude, exact Moon phase & illumination %, upcoming New/Full Moon instants in your selected timezone, and Lahiri sidereal Moon calculation.
   - **Choose My Lens:** Browse manually, compute Western Tropical Sun, or Vedic Lahiri Moon with city presets (including **Kolkata, Kalyani, Chakdaha**) and first-class **"I don't know birth time"** boundary checks across the civil day, plus a **"How this was found" JSON Fact Packet** drawer.
3. **Complete UNSENT Experience:**
   - **Today:** Quiet daily greeting, 7-day strip, primary **Write a letter** & **Make a feeling card** actions, **Dreamer's Almanac** monthly lunar calendar with linked notes, and daily reflection.
   - **Zodiac:** 3D Wheel, 4 Elements 3D explorer, searchable 12-sign gallery (each with 7 distinct prompts + 2-minute action), Choose My Lens, and Two-Sign Conversation Cards (no percentages or marriage verdicts).
   - **Studio (Letters & 9:16 Cards):** 9 recipients with 5 prompts each, 6 tactile paper themes (*Old Parchment, Midnight Blue, Rose Petal, Rain on Glass, Torn Notebook, Moonlit Ink*), autosave to IndexedDB, dated **`NEVER SENT`** ink stamp, 9:16 **1080×1920 PNG export** with safe-area/font controls, and Web Share fallback.
   - **My Box & Settings:** Private local archive with search, mood check-in recap, removable demo entries, **EN / বাংলা / हिन्दी / Hinglish** localization, **Midnight / Daylight Paper** themes, **Text Size**, **Reduce Motion**, JSON vault export/import, and a calm **Find human support** resource drawer.

/* ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   2. DETERMINISTIC ASTRONOMY ENGINE (MEEUS SOLAR & LUNAR)
   Zero LLM math. Returns validated Fact Packets.
═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */
const RAD = Math.PI / 180;
const mod360 = x => ((x % 360) + 360) % 360;

function toJulianDay(date) {
  return date.getTime() / 86400000 + 2440587.5;
}

function getSolarTropicalLongitude(date) {
  const T = (toJulianDay(date) - 2451545.0) / 36525.0;
  const L0 = 280.46646 + 36000.76983 * T + 0.0003032 * T * T;
  const M = (357.52911 + 35999.05029 * T - 0.0001537 * T * T) * RAD;
  const C = (1.914602 - 0.004817 * T - 0.000014 * T * T) * Math.sin(M)
          + (0.019993 - 0.000101 * T) * Math.sin(2 * M)
          + 0.000289 * Math.sin(3 * M);
  const omega = (125.04 - 1934.136 * T) * RAD;
  return mod360(L0 + C - 0.00569 - 0.00478 * Math.sin(omega));
}

function getLunarLongitude(date) {
  const T = (toJulianDay(date) - 2451545.0) / 36525.0;
  const Lp = 218.3164477 + 481267.88123421 * T - 0.0015786 * T * T;
  const D  = (297.8501921 + 445267.1114034 * T - 0.0018819 * T * T) * RAD;
  const M  = (357.5291092 + 35999.0502909 * T) * RAD;
  const Mp = (134.9633964 + 477198.8675055 * T + 0.0087414 * T * T) * RAD;
  const F  = (93.2720950  + 483202.0175233 * T) * RAD;

  const sigmaL =
      6.288774 * Math.sin(Mp)
    + 1.274027 * Math.sin(2 * D - Mp)
    + 0.658314 * Math.sin(2 * D)
    + 0.213618 * Math.sin(2 * Mp)
    - 0.185116 * Math.sin(M)
    - 0.114332 * Math.sin(2 * F)
    + 0.058793 * Math.sin(2 * D - 2 * Mp)
    + 0.057066 * Math.sin(2 * D - M - Mp)
    + 0.053322 * Math.sin(2 * D + Mp)
    + 0.045758 * Math.sin(2 * D - M)
    - 0.040923 * Math.sin(M - Mp)
    - 0.034720 * Math.sin(D)
    - 0.030383 * Math.sin(M + Mp);

  return mod360(Lp + sigmaL);
}

function getLahiriAyanamsa(date) {
  const T = (toJulianDay(date) - 2451545.0) / 36525.0;
  return 23.853056 + 1.396971 * T;
}

function getMoonPhaseInfo(date) {
  const sun = getSolarTropicalLongitude(date);
  const moon = getLunarLongitude(date);
  const angle = mod360(moon - sun);
  const illum = Math.round(((1 - Math.cos(angle * RAD)) / 2) * 100);

  let name = 'Waxing Crescent';
  if (angle < 11.25 || angle >= 348.75) name = 'New Moon';
  else if (angle < 78.75) name = 'Waxing Crescent';
  else if (angle < 101.25) name = 'First Quarter';
  else if (angle < 168.75) name = 'Waxing Gibbous';
  else if (angle < 191.25) name = 'Full Moon';
  else if (angle < 258.75) name = 'Waning Gibbous';
  else if (angle < 281.25) name = 'Last Quarter';
  else name = 'Waning Crescent';

  return { angle: +angle.toFixed(2), illumination: illum, phaseName: name };
}

function findNextMoonEvent(startDate, targetAngle) {
  const stepMs = 6 * 3600 * 1000;
  let t1 = startDate.getTime();
  const signedDiff = (t) => {
    const a = mod360(getLunarLongitude(new Date(t)) - getSolarTropicalLongitude(new Date(t)));
    let d = a - targetAngle;
    if (d > 180) d -= 360;
    if (d < -180) d += 360;
    return d;
  };
  let d1 = signedDiff(t1);
  for (let i = 0; i < 140; i++) {
    const t2 = t1 + stepMs;
    const d2 = signedDiff(t2);
    if (d1 <= 0 && d2 >= 0 && Math.abs(d2 - d1) < 90) {
      let lo = t1, hi = t2;
      for (let b = 0; b < 18; b++) {
        const mid = (lo + hi) / 2;
        if (signedDiff(mid) < 0) lo = mid; else hi = mid;
      }
      return new Date((lo + hi) / 2);
    }
    t1 = t2;
    d1 = d2;
  }
  return null;
}

function signFromLongitude(deg) {
  const idx = Math.floor(mod360(deg) / 30);
  return SIGNS[idx];
}

function createFactPacket(opts) {
  return {
    schemaVersion: '1.0.0',
    engine: 'Meeus-JS-Deterministic-Ephemeris',
    ...opts
  };
}

   3. STATE & INDEXEDDB + LOCAL STORAGE PERSISTENCE
═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */
const STATE_KEY = 'unsent_astral_state_v1';
let db = null;

const state = {
  lensSignId: 'pisces',
  lensSource: 'Chosen lens',
  selectedZodiacSign: 'pisces',
  activeElement3D: 'Fire',
  zodiacView: 'elements',
  pairA: 'gemini',
  pairB: 'pisces',
  dialogueCtx: 'friendship',
  lang: 'en',
  theme: 'dark',
  reduceMotion: false,
  textSize: '1',
  timezone: 'Asia/Kolkata',
  sparkOffsetToday: 0,
  sparkOffsetDetail: 0,
  recipientId: 'friend',
  recipientPromptIdx: 0,
  paperId: 'parchment',
  cardTheme: 'obsidian',
  studioTab: 'letter',
  boxFilter: 'all',
  vaultLocked: false,
  hideSampleCard: false,
  releaseMode: 'birds',
  selectedAlmanacDate: null,
  lastFactPacket: null,
  items: [], // Letters & Feeling Cards
  checkins: [] // Emotional weather & Almanac linked notes
};

function openIDB() {
  return new Promise((resolve) => {
    if (!window.indexedDB) { resolve(null); return; }
    const req = indexedDB.open('unsent_astral_vault', 1);
    req.onupgradeneeded = () => {
      const d = req.result;
      if (!d.objectStoreNames.contains('vault')) d.createObjectStore('vault', { keyPath: 'id' });
    };
    req.onsuccess = () => resolve(req.result);
    req.onerror = () => resolve(null);
  });
}

function loadState() {
  try {
    const raw = localStorage.getItem(STATE_KEY);
    if (raw) Object.assign(state, JSON.parse(raw));
  } catch {}
}

function persistState() {
  try {
    const { lastFactPacket, ...serializable } = state;
    localStorage.setItem(STATE_KEY, JSON.stringify(serializable));
    if (db) {
      const tx = db.transaction('vault', 'readwrite');
      tx.objectStore('vault').put({ id: 'main_archive', payload: serializable, updatedAt: Date.now() });
    }
  } catch {}
}

function getSign(id) {
  return SIGNS.find(s => s.id === id) || SIGNS[11];
}

/* ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   4. THREE.JS 3D ELEMENTS & CELESTIAL VISUAL ENGINE
═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */
const threeManagers = {};

function createStarParticles(count, spread, colorHex, size = 0.05) {
  const geo = new THREE.BufferGeometry();
  const arr = new Float32Array(count * 3);
  for (let i = 0; i < count * 3; i++) arr[i] = (Math.random() - 0.5) * spread;
  geo.setAttribute('position', new THREE.BufferAttribute(arr, 3));
  return new THREE.Points(geo, new THREE.PointsMaterial({ color: colorHex, size, transparent: true, opacity: 0.72 }));
}

/* Build True 3D Sculptures for Fire, Earth, Air, Water */
function build3DElementSculpture(elementName) {
  const group = new THREE.Group();
  group.userData.element = elementName;

  if (elementName === 'Fire') {
    // Multi-layered flickering flame lobes + rising spiral embers
    const makeFlameLobe = (sx, sy, sz, color, opacity, yOff = 0) => {
      const geo = new THREE.ConeGeometry(0.62, 1.55, 28, 16, true);
      const pos = geo.attributes.position;
      const basePos = Float32Array.from(pos.array);
      geo.userData.base = basePos;
      const mat = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({
        color,
        emissive: color,
        emissiveIntensity: 0.85,
        roughness: 0.35,
        transparent: true,
        opacity,
        side: THREE.DoubleSide
      });
      const mesh = new THREE.Mesh(geo, mat);
      mesh.scale.set(sx, sy, sz);
      mesh.position.y = yOff;
      return mesh;
    };

    const outer = makeFlameLobe(1.05, 1.05, 1.05, 0xE85334, 0.72, 0.05);
    const mid = makeFlameLobe(0.75, 0.92, 0.75, 0xF59D42, 0.86, -0.05);
    const core = makeFlameLobe(0.45, 0.72, 0.45, 0xFFF2B2, 0.95, -0.18);

    // Volcanic obsidian base ring
    const baseRing = new THREE.Mesh(
      new THREE.TorusGeometry(0.95, 0.03, 12, 64),
      new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: 0xD9B26F, metalness: 0.6, roughness: 0.3 })
    );
    baseRing.rotation.x = Math.PI / 2;
    baseRing.position.y = -0.75;

    // Rising ember particles
    const emberN = 120;
    const emberGeo = new THREE.BufferGeometry();
    const emberPos = new Float32Array(emberN * 3);
    for (let i = 0; i < emberN; i++) {
      const ang = Math.random() * Math.PI * 2;
      const rad = 0.2 + Math.random() * 0.9;
      emberPos[i * 3] = Math.cos(ang) * rad;
      emberPos[i * 3 + 1] = -0.8 + Math.random() * 2.1;
      emberPos[i * 3 + 2] = Math.sin(ang) * rad;
    }
    emberGeo.setAttribute('position', new THREE.BufferAttribute(emberPos, 3));
    const embers = new THREE.Points(emberGeo, new THREE.PointsMaterial({ color: 0xFFC56D, size: 0.055, transparent: true, opacity: 0.9 }));

    const glow = new THREE.PointLight(0xFF7B3D, 4.5, 8);
    glow.position.set(0, 0.2, 0.6);

    group.add(outer, mid, core, baseRing, embers, glow);
    group.userData.update = (t) => {
      [outer, mid, core].forEach((m, idx) => {
        const pos = m.geometry.attributes.position;
        const base = m.geometry.userData.base;
        for (let i = 0; i < pos.count; i++) {
          const bx = base[i * 3], by = base[i * 3 + 1], bz = base[i * 3 + 2];
          const hNorm = Math.max(0, (by + 0.77) / 1.55);
          const sway = Math.sin(t * (3.2 + idx) + by * 3.5 + bx * 2) * 0.14 * hNorm;
          const swayZ = Math.cos(t * (2.6 + idx) + by * 2.8) * 0.11 * hNorm;
          pos.setXYZ(i, bx + sway, by, bz + swayZ);
        }
        pos.needsUpdate = true;
      });
      const ep = embers.geometry.attributes.position;
      for (let i = 0; i < emberN; i++) {
        let y = ep.getY(i) + 0.015;
        if (y > 1.35) y = -0.75;
        const ang = t * 0.8 + i;
        ep.setY(i, y);
        ep.setX(i, ep.getX(i) + Math.sin(ang) * 0.004);
      }
      ep.needsUpdate = true;
      group.rotation.y = t * 0.25;
    };
  }

  else if (elementName === 'Earth') {
    // Sculpted 3D Mountain Peaks + Full Moon behind + Orbiting Mineral Shards
    const makePeak = (r, h, seg, col, x, y, z) => {
      const geo = new THREE.ConeGeometry(r, h, seg, 2);
      const pos = geo.attributes.position;
      for (let i = 0; i < pos.count; i++) {
        if (pos.getY(i) < h * 0.4) {
          pos.setX(i, pos.getX(i) * (0.85 + ((i * 19) % 10) * 0.03));
          pos.setZ(i, pos.getZ(i) * (0.85 + ((i * 23) % 10) * 0.03));
        }
      }
      geo.computeVertexNormals();
      const mesh = new THREE.Mesh(geo, new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({
        color: col,
        roughness: 0.72,
        metalness: 0.15,
        flatShading: true
      }));
      mesh.position.set(x, y, z);
      return mesh;
    };

    const mainPeak = makePeak(0.75, 1.35, 7, 0x528865, 0, -0.05, 0.1);
    const leftPeak = makePeak(0.55, 0.95, 6, 0x39634B, -0.56, -0.25, -0.08);
    const rightPeak = makePeak(0.52, 0.88, 6, 0x6AA37A, 0.54, -0.28, -0.06);

    // Moon behind mountain (Image 3 Earth motif!)
    const moon = new THREE.Mesh(
      new THREE.SphereGeometry(0.45, 28, 28),
      new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: 0xF5EAD6, emissive: 0xD9B26F, emissiveIntensity: 0.45, roughness: 0.5 })
    );
    moon.position.set(0, 0.48, -0.58);

    // Terraced stone/gold base rings
    const ring1 = new THREE.Mesh(
      new THREE.TorusGeometry(1.05, 0.025, 10, 64),
      new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: 0xD9B26F, metalness: 0.65, roughness: 0.35 })
    );
    ring1.rotation.x = Math.PI / 2;
    ring1.position.y = -0.72;

    // Orbiting emerald mineral shards
    const shards = new THREE.Group();
    for (let i = 0; i < 8; i++) {
      const a = (i / 8) * Math.PI * 2;
      const sh = new THREE.Mesh(
        new THREE.OctahedronGeometry(0.085, 0),
        new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: 0x8FD19E, emissive: 0x366B48, emissiveIntensity: 0.35, roughness: 0.2, metalness: 0.3 })
      );
      sh.position.set(Math.cos(a) * 1.12, -0.3 + (i % 3) * 0.25, Math.sin(a) * 1.12);
      shards.add(sh);
    }

    group.add(mainPeak, leftPeak, rightPeak, moon, ring1, shards);
    group.userData.update = (t) => {
      group.rotation.y = Math.sin(t * 0.35) * 0.45;
      shards.rotation.y = t * 0.45;
      shards.children.forEach((c, idx) => { c.rotation.x = t + idx; c.rotation.y = t * 0.8; });
    };
  }

  else if (elementName === 'Air') {
    // Sweeping 3D Helical Wind Ribbons + Cloud Wisps + Star Dust
    const ribbons = new THREE.Group();
    [0, 1, 2].forEach((idx) => {
      const pts = [];
      for (let i = 0; i <= 42; i++) {
        const u = i / 42;
        const angle = u * Math.PI * 3.2 + idx * ((Math.PI * 2) / 3);
        const radius = 0.28 + Math.sin(u * Math.PI) * 0.78;
        pts.push(new THREE.Vector3(
          Math.cos(angle) * radius,
          (u - 0.5) * 1.55,
          Math.sin(angle) * radius
        ));
      }
      const curve = new THREE.CatmullRomCurve3(pts);
      const tubeGeo = new THREE.TubeGeometry(curve, 64, 0.032 - idx * 0.005, 8, false);
      const tubeMat = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({
        color: idx === 0 ? 0x9BD2F5 : idx === 1 ? 0xD8EEFC : 0x6EAEE0,
        emissive: 0x4E8FC7,
        emissiveIntensity: 0.45,
        roughness: 0.25,
        metalness: 0.2,
        transparent: true,
        opacity: 0.88
      });
      ribbons.add(new THREE.Mesh(tubeGeo, tubeMat));
    });

    // Soft floating cloud puffs
    const clouds = new THREE.Group();
    [[-0.55, 0.35, 0.2, 0.22], [0.5, -0.25, 0.25, 0.2], [0.1, 0.6, -0.2, 0.18]].forEach(([x, y, z, r]) => {
      const puff = new THREE.Mesh(
        new THREE.SphereGeometry(r, 18, 18),
        new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: 0xEBF5FD, roughness: 0.8, transparent: true, opacity: 0.75 })
      );
      puff.scale.set(1.4, 0.75, 1.0);
      puff.position.set(x, y, z);
      clouds.add(puff);
    });

    const windDust = createStarParticles(120, 2.5, 0xC6E6FB, 0.045);
    group.add(ribbons, clouds, windDust);
    group.userData.update = (t) => {
      ribbons.rotation.y = t * 0.75;
      clouds.rotation.y = -t * 0.25;
      clouds.children.forEach((c, idx) => { c.position.y += Math.sin(t * 1.4 + idx) * 0.0018; });
      windDust.rotation.y = t * 0.45;
    };
  }

  else {
    // Water: Animated 3D Undulating Ocean Surface + Crescent Moon + Mist Droplets
    const waveGeo = new THREE.PlaneGeometry(2.2, 2.2, 34, 34);
    waveGeo.rotateX(-Math.PI / 2);
    const baseY = Float32Array.from(waveGeo.attributes.position.array);
    waveGeo.userData.base = baseY;

    const waveMat = new THREE.MeshPhysicalMaterial({
      color: 0x2456A8,
      emissive: 0x102854,
      emissiveIntensity: 0.55,
      roughness: 0.12,
      metalness: 0.15,
      clearcoat: 1.0,
      clearcoatRoughness: 0.08,
      transparent: true,
      opacity: 0.92,
      side: THREE.DoubleSide
    });
    const ocean = new THREE.Mesh(waveGeo, waveMat);
    ocean.position.y = -0.35;

    // Outer wave ring frame
    const ring = new THREE.Mesh(
      new THREE.TorusGeometry(1.12, 0.024, 10, 64),
      new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: 0xD9B26F, metalness: 0.65, roughness: 0.3 })
    );
    ring.rotation.x = Math.PI / 2;
    ring.position.y = -0.35;

    // Crescent Moon above waves (carved using inner mask or sculpted crescent torus arc)
    const crescent = new THREE.Mesh(
      new THREE.TorusGeometry(0.36, 0.095, 16, 48, Math.PI * 1.25),
      new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: 0xF5EAD6, emissive: 0xD9B26F, emissiveIntensity: 0.55, roughness: 0.3 })
    );
    crescent.position.set(0.1, 0.62, -0.35);
    crescent.rotation.z = Math.PI * 0.62;

    const droplets = createStarParticles(90, 2.2, 0x9BC3F7, 0.05);
    droplets.position.y = 0.2;

    const moonLight = new THREE.PointLight(0x88B8FF, 3.5, 8);
    moonLight.position.set(0, 1.0, 0.8);

    group.add(ocean, ring, crescent, droplets, moonLight);
    group.userData.update = (t) => {
      const pos = ocean.geometry.attributes.position;
      const base = ocean.geometry.userData.base;
      for (let i = 0; i < pos.count; i++) {
        const bx = base[i * 3], bz = base[i * 3 + 2];
        const dist = Math.hypot(bx, bz);
        if (dist > 1.12) {
          pos.setY(i, -0.15);
          continue;
        }
        const wave = Math.sin(bx * 3.2 + t * 2.1) * 0.13 + Math.cos(bz * 3.6 - t * 1.7) * 0.11;
        pos.setY(i, wave * (1 - dist / 1.35));
      }
      pos.needsUpdate = true;
      ocean.geometry.computeVertexNormals();
      crescent.position.y = 0.62 + Math.sin(t * 1.1) * 0.05;
      droplets.rotation.y = t * 0.25;
      group.rotation.y = Math.sin(t * 0.28) * 0.3;
    };
  }

  return group;
}

/* Init Scene 1: Today 3D Sanctuary (Active Sign Element + Lunar Phase Orb + Starfield) */
function initTodayStage() {
  const canvas = document.getElementById('canvas-today');
  const wrap = canvas.parentElement;
  const renderer = new THREE.WebGLRenderer({ canvas, antialias: true, alpha: true });
  renderer.setPixelRatio(Math.min(window.devicePixelRatio, 2));
  renderer.setSize(wrap.clientWidth, wrap.clientHeight, false);

  const scene = new THREE.Scene();
  const camera = new THREE.PerspectiveCamera(45, wrap.clientWidth / wrap.clientHeight, 0.1, 100);
  camera.position.set(0, 0.35, 3.85);

  scene.add(createStarParticles(380, 16, 0xD9B26F, 0.04));
  scene.add(new THREE.AmbientLight(0xffffff, 0.75));
  const key = new THREE.DirectionalLight(0xFFF3DB, 2.1);
  key.position.set(2.5, 2.2, 2.8);
  scene.add(key);

  // Orbiting Lunar Phase Moon Orb
  const moon = new THREE.Mesh(
    new THREE.SphereGeometry(0.28, 28, 28),
    new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: 0xF5EAD6, roughness: 0.78 })
  );
  moon.position.set(1.25, 0.72, -0.3);
  scene.add(moon);

  const astrolabeRing = new THREE.Mesh(
    new THREE.TorusGeometry(1.38, 0.012, 8, 96),
    new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: 0xD9B26F, metalness: 0.7, roughness: 0.3, transparent: true, opacity: 0.55 })
  );
  astrolabeRing.rotation.x = Math.PI / 2.6;
  scene.add(astrolabeRing);

  const elemHolder = new THREE.Group();
  elemHolder.scale.setScalar(0.88);
  scene.add(elemHolder);

  let dragY = 0, dragX = 0, down = false, lx = 0, ly = 0;
  wrap.addEventListener('pointerdown', e => { down = true; lx = e.clientX; ly = e.clientY; });
  window.addEventListener('pointerup', () => { down = false; });
  wrap.addEventListener('pointermove', e => {
    if (!down) return;
    dragY += (e.clientX - lx) * 0.008;
    dragX += (e.clientY - ly) * 0.005;
    lx = e.clientX; ly = e.clientY;
  });

  threeManagers.today = {
    renderer, scene, camera, elemHolder, astrolabeRing, moon,
    currentElem: null,
    getDrag: () => ({ x: dragX, y: dragY })
  };
  syncTodayElement3D();
}

function syncTodayElement3D() {
  const m = threeManagers.today;
  if (!m) return;
  const sign = getSign(state.lensSignId);
  if (m.currentElem !== sign.element) {
    while (m.elemHolder.children.length) m.elemHolder.remove(m.elemHolder.children[0]);
    m.elemHolder.add(build3DElementSculpture(sign.element));
    m.currentElem = sign.element;
  }
}

/* Init Scene 2: 4 Elements Interactive 3D Stage */
function initElementStage() {
  const canvas = document.getElementById('canvas-element');
  const wrap = canvas.parentElement;
  const renderer = new THREE.WebGLRenderer({ canvas, antialias: true, alpha: true });
  renderer.setPixelRatio(Math.min(window.devicePixelRatio, 2));
  renderer.setSize(wrap.clientWidth || 360, wrap.clientHeight || 235, false);

  const scene = new THREE.Scene();
  const camera = new THREE.PerspectiveCamera(44, (wrap.clientWidth || 360) / (wrap.clientHeight || 235), 0.1, 100);
  camera.position.set(0, 0.35, 3.6);

  scene.add(createStarParticles(350, 15, 0xB8B4CC, 0.04));
  scene.add(new THREE.AmbientLight(0xffffff, 0.85));
  const dl = new THREE.DirectionalLight(0xFFF6E6, 2.0);
  dl.position.set(2, 3, 2);
  scene.add(dl);

  const holder = new THREE.Group();
  scene.add(holder);

  threeManagers.element = { renderer, scene, camera, holder, currentElem: null };
  syncElementStage3D();
}

function syncElementStage3D() {
  const m = threeManagers.element;
  if (!m) return;
  if (m.currentElem !== state.activeElement3D) {
    while (m.holder.children.length) m.holder.remove(m.holder.children[0]);
    m.holder.add(build3DElementSculpture(state.activeElement3D));
    m.currentElem = state.activeElement3D;
  }
}

/* Init Scene 3: 3D Celestial Zodiac Astrolabe Wheel */
function initWheelStage() {
  const canvas = document.getElementById('canvas-wheel');
  const wrap = canvas.parentElement;
  const renderer = new THREE.WebGLRenderer({ canvas, antialias: true, alpha: true });
  renderer.setPixelRatio(Math.min(window.devicePixelRatio, 2));
  renderer.setSize(wrap.clientWidth || 360, wrap.clientHeight || 260, false);

  const scene = new THREE.Scene();
  const camera = new THREE.PerspectiveCamera(45, (wrap.clientWidth || 360) / (wrap.clientHeight || 260), 0.1, 100);
  camera.position.set(0, 1.85, 4.15);
  camera.lookAt(0, 0, 0);

  scene.add(createStarParticles(400, 16, 0xD9B26F, 0.045));
  scene.add(new THREE.AmbientLight(0xffffff, 0.8));
  const sunLight = new THREE.PointLight(0xFFD98E, 4, 12);
  sunLight.position.set(0, 0.8, 0);
  scene.add(sunLight);

  const wheel = new THREE.Group();
  // Radiant Golden Sun Core
  const sun = new THREE.Mesh(
    new THREE.SphereGeometry(0.32, 24, 24),
    new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: 0xFFE6A3, emissive: 0xD99B38, emissiveIntensity: 0.9 })
  );
  wheel.add(sun);

  // Sun Rays
  for (let i = 0; i < 12; i++) {
    const a = (i / 12) * Math.PI * 2;
    const ray = new THREE.Mesh(
      new THREE.ConeGeometry(0.06, 0.34, 4),
      new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: 0xD9B26F, emissive: 0xD9B26F, emissiveIntensity: 0.5 })
    );
    ray.position.set(Math.sin(a) * 0.44, 0, Math.cos(a) * 0.44);
    ray.rotation.z = -Math.sin(a) * (Math.PI / 2);
    ray.rotation.x = Math.cos(a) * (Math.PI / 2);
    wheel.add(ray);
  }

  // Concentric Gold Astrolabe Rings
  [1.05, 1.65, 1.92].forEach((r, idx) => {
    const ring = new THREE.Mesh(
      new THREE.TorusGeometry(r, idx === 1 ? 0.025 : 0.012, 8, 96),
      new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: 0xD9B26F, metalness: 0.7, roughness: 0.28 })
    );
    ring.rotation.x = Math.PI / 2;
    wheel.add(ring);
  });

  // 12 Zodiac Sign Nodes + Spokes
  const nodes = [];
  SIGNS.forEach((s, i) => {
    const a = (i / 12) * Math.PI * 2;
    const node = new THREE.Mesh(
      new THREE.OctahedronGeometry(0.14, 0),
      new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({
        color: s.color,
        emissive: s.color,
        emissiveIntensity: 0.35,
        roughness: 0.2,
        metalness: 0.3
      })
    );
    node.position.set(Math.sin(a) * 1.65, 0.06, Math.cos(a) * 1.65);
    wheel.add(node);

    const spokeGeo = new THREE.BufferGeometry().setFromPoints([
      new THREE.Vector3(Math.sin(a) * 1.05, 0, Math.cos(a) * 1.05),
      new THREE.Vector3(Math.sin(a) * 1.92, 0, Math.cos(a) * 1.92)
    ]);
    wheel.add(new THREE.Line(spokeGeo, new THREE.LineBasicMaterial({ color: 0xD9B26F, transparent: true, opacity: 0.35 })));
    nodes.push({ id: s.id, mesh: node });
  });

  scene.add(wheel);
  threeManagers.wheel = { renderer, scene, camera, wheel, nodes, targetRot: 0 };
  syncWheelTarget();
}

function syncWheelTarget() {
  const m = threeManagers.wheel;
  if (!m) return;
  const idx = SIGNS.findIndex(s => s.id === state.selectedZodiacSign);
  m.targetRot = -(idx / 12) * Math.PI * 2;
  m.nodes.forEach(n => {
    const active = n.id === state.selectedZodiacSign;
    n.mesh.scale.setScalar(active ? 1.55 : 0.92);
    n.mesh.material.emissiveIntensity = active ? 0.95 : 0.25;
  });
}

/* Init Scene 4: Two-Sign Elemental Dialogue */
function initAlchemyStage() {
  const canvas = document.getElementById('canvas-alchemy');
  const wrap = canvas.parentElement;
  const renderer = new THREE.WebGLRenderer({ canvas, antialias: true, alpha: true });
  renderer.setPixelRatio(Math.min(window.devicePixelRatio, 2));
  renderer.setSize(wrap.clientWidth || 360, wrap.clientHeight || 200, false);

  const scene = new THREE.Scene();
  const camera = new THREE.PerspectiveCamera(45, (wrap.clientWidth || 360) / (wrap.clientHeight || 200), 0.1, 100);
  camera.position.set(0, 0.3, 4.4);

  scene.add(createStarParticles(250, 14, 0xD9B26F, 0.04));
  scene.add(new THREE.AmbientLight(0xffffff, 0.85));
  const dl = new THREE.DirectionalLight(0xffffff, 1.8);
  dl.position.set(1, 3, 2);
  scene.add(dl);

  const holderA = new THREE.Group();
  const holderB = new THREE.Group();
  holderA.scale.setScalar(0.62);
  holderB.scale.setScalar(0.62);
  holderA.position.set(-1.15, 0, 0);
  holderB.position.set(1.15, 0, 0);

  const ring = new THREE.Mesh(
    new THREE.TorusGeometry(1.25, 0.01, 8, 84),
    new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ color: 0xD9B26F, transparent: true, opacity: 0.35 })
  );
  ring.rotation.x = Math.PI / 2.2;

  scene.add(holderA, holderB, ring);
  threeManagers.alchemy = { renderer, scene, camera, holderA, holderB, key: '' };
  syncAlchemy3D();
}

function syncAlchemy3D() {
  const m = threeManagers.alchemy;
  if (!m) return;
  const sa = getSign(state.pairA), sb = getSign(state.pairB);
  const key = `${sa.element}_${sb.element}`;
  if (m.key !== key) {
    while (m.holderA.children.length) m.holderA.remove(m.holderA.children[0]);
    while (m.holderB.children.length) m.holderB.remove(m.holderB.children[0]);
    m.holderA.add(build3DElementSculpture(sa.element));
    m.holderB.add(build3DElementSculpture(sb.element));
    m.key = key;
  }
}

  }
}

/* Init Scene 6: Release Ritual 3D (Paper Birds / Embers / Ash) */
function initRelease3D() {
  const canvas = document.getElementById('canvas-release');
  const wrap = canvas.parentElement;
  const renderer = new THREE.WebGLRenderer({ canvas, antialias: true, alpha: true });
  renderer.setPixelRatio(Math.min(window.devicePixelRatio, 2));
  renderer.setSize(wrap.clientWidth || 340, wrap.clientHeight || 175, false);

  const scene = new THREE.Scene();
  const camera = new THREE.PerspectiveCamera(48, (wrap.clientWidth || 340) / (wrap.clientHeight || 175), 0.1, 100);
  camera.position.set(0, 0, 4.2);

  scene.add(new THREE.AmbientLight(0xffffff, 0.9));
  const holder = new THREE.Group();
  scene.add(holder);

  threeManagers.release = { renderer, scene, camera, holder, mode: null };
  rebuildReleaseParticles();
}

function rebuildReleaseParticles() {
  const m = threeManagers.release;
  if (!m) return;
  while (m.holder.children.length) m.holder.remove(m.holder.children[0]);

  if (state.releaseMode === 'birds') {
    for (let i = 0; i < 16; i++) {
      const bird = new THREE.Group();
      const wingGeo = new THREE.BufferGeometry();
      wingGeo.setAttribute('position', new THREE.BufferAttribute(new Float32Array([
        0, 0, 0.12,   0.22, 0.05, 0,   0, 0, -0.14
      ]), 3));
      const mat = new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ color: 0xF5EAD6, side: THREE.DoubleSide });
      const leftW = new THREE.Mesh(wingGeo, mat);
      const rightW = new THREE.Mesh(wingGeo, mat);
      rightW.scale.x = -1;
      bird.add(leftW, rightW);
      bird.position.set((Math.random() - 0.5) * 2.8, -1.2 + Math.random() * 2.4, (Math.random() - 0.5) * 1.5);
      bird.userData = { leftW, rightW, phase: Math.random() * Math.PI * 2, speed: 0.012 + Math.random() * 0.01 };
      m.holder.add(bird);
    }
  } else {
    const isEmber = state.releaseMode === 'embers';
    const n = 220;
    const geo = new THREE.BufferGeometry();
    const pos = new Float32Array(n * 3);
    for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
      pos[i * 3] = (Math.random() - 0.5) * 3.8;
      pos[i * 3 + 1] = (Math.random() - 0.5) * 2.4;
      pos[i * 3 + 2] = (Math.random() - 0.5) * 1.6;
    }
    geo.setAttribute('position', new THREE.BufferAttribute(pos, 3));
    const pts = new THREE.Points(geo, new THREE.PointsMaterial({
      color: isEmber ? 0xFF8C42 : 0xB8B5C4,
      size: isEmber ? 0.065 : 0.05,
      transparent: true,
      opacity: 0.85
    }));
    pts.userData.isEmber = isEmber;
    m.holder.add(pts);
  }
}

/* Init Scene 7: Desktop Companion Panel 3D */
function initDesktopCompanion3D() {
  const canvas = document.getElementById('canvas-desktop');
  if (!canvas || window.innerWidth < 980) return;
  const wrap = canvas.parentElement;
  const renderer = new THREE.WebGLRenderer({ canvas, antialias: true, alpha: true });

  const scene = new THREE.Scene();
  const camera = new THREE.PerspectiveCamera(44, wrap.clientWidth / wrap.clientHeight, 0.1, 100);

  scene.add(createStarParticles(520, 18, 0xD9B26F, 0.04));
  scene.add(new THREE.AmbientLight(0xffffff, 0.8));
  const dl = new THREE.DirectionalLight(0xFFF2DA, 2.0);
  dl.position.set(2, 3, 2);
  scene.add(dl);

  const holder = new THREE.Group();
  scene.add(holder);

  threeManagers.desktop = { renderer, scene, camera, holder, currentElem: null };

function syncDesktopCompanion() {
  const sign = getSign(state.lensSignId);
  const el = ELEMENTS_DATA[sign.element];
  document.getElementById('dc-title').textContent = `${sign.element} · ${sign.western} (${sign.rashi})`;
  document.getElementById('dc-themes-text').textContent = el.themes;
  document.getElementById('dc-reflection-text').textContent = el.reflection;

  const m = threeManagers.desktop;
  if (!m) return;
  if (m.currentElem !== sign.element) {
    while (m.holder.children.length) m.holder.remove(m.holder.children[0]);
    m.holder.add(build3DElementSculpture(sign.element));
    m.currentElem = sign.element;
  }
}

/* Unified 60fps Loop */
let animT = 0;
function loop3D() {
  requestAnimationFrame(loop3D);
  if (!state.reduceMotion) animT += 0.014;

  // Today stage
  if (threeManagers.today && document.getElementById('s-today').classList.contains('active')) {
    const m = threeManagers.today;
    const d = m.getDrag();
    m.elemHolder.children.forEach(c => c.userData.update?.(animT));
    m.elemHolder.rotation.y = d.y;
    m.elemHolder.rotation.x = d.x * 0.4;
    if (!state.reduceMotion) m.astrolabeRing.rotation.z = animT * 0.16;
    m.renderer.render(m.scene, m.camera);
  }

  // Zodiac stages
  if (document.getElementById('s-zodiac').classList.contains('active')) {
    if (state.zodiacView === 'elements' && threeManagers.element) {
      const m = threeManagers.element;
      m.holder.children.forEach(c => c.userData.update?.(animT));
      m.renderer.render(m.scene, m.camera);
    }
    if (state.zodiacView === 'wheel' && threeManagers.wheel) {
      const m = threeManagers.wheel;
      m.wheel.rotation.y += (m.targetRot - m.wheel.rotation.y) * (state.reduceMotion ? 1 : 0.09);
      m.renderer.render(m.scene, m.camera);
    }
    if (state.zodiacView === 'dialogue' && threeManagers.alchemy) {
      const m = threeManagers.alchemy;
      m.holderA.children.forEach(c => c.userData.update?.(animT));
      m.holderB.children.forEach(c => c.userData.update?.(animT + 1.2));
      m.renderer.render(m.scene, m.camera);
    }
  }

  // Studio 9:16 card stage
  if (threeManagers.card && document.getElementById('s-studio').classList.contains('active') && state.studioTab === 'card') {
    const m = threeManagers.card;
    m.holder.children.forEach(c => c.userData.update?.(animT));
    m.renderer.render(m.scene, m.camera);
  }

  // Release sheet stage
  if (threeManagers.release && document.getElementById('sh-release').classList.contains('open')) {
    const m = threeManagers.release;
    if (!state.reduceMotion) {
      m.holder.children.forEach(obj => {
        if (obj.userData.leftW) {
          const flap = Math.sin(animT * 6 + obj.userData.phase) * 0.45;
          obj.userData.leftW.rotation.z = flap;
          obj.userData.rightW.rotation.z = -flap;
          obj.position.y += obj.userData.speed;
          obj.position.x += Math.sin(animT + obj.userData.phase) * 0.005;
          if (obj.position.y > 1.35) obj.position.y = -1.25;
        } else if (obj.geometry?.attributes?.position) {
          const pos = obj.geometry.attributes.position;
          const dir = obj.userData.isEmber ? 0.014 : -0.009;
          for (let i = 0; i < pos.count; i++) {
            let y = pos.getY(i) + dir;
            if (dir > 0 && y > 1.25) y = -1.25;
            if (dir < 0 && y < -1.25) y = 1.25;
            pos.setY(i, y);
            pos.setX(i, pos.getX(i) + Math.sin(animT + i) * 0.002);
          }
          pos.needsUpdate = true;
        }
      });
    }
    m.renderer.render(m.scene, m.camera);
  }

  // Desktop companion
  if (threeManagers.desktop && window.innerWidth >= 980) {
    const m = threeManagers.desktop;
    m.holder.children.forEach(c => c.userData.update?.(animT));
    m.renderer.render(m.scene, m.camera);
  }
}

function resize3D() {
  Object.values(threeManagers).forEach(m => {
    if (!m?.renderer) return;
    const wrap = m.renderer.domElement.parentElement;
    if (!wrap || !wrap.clientWidth || !wrap.clientHeight) return;
    m.camera.aspect = wrap.clientWidth / wrap.clientHeight;
    m.camera.updateProjectionMatrix();
    m.renderer.setSize(wrap.clientWidth, wrap.clientHeight, false);
  });
}

/* ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   5. NAVIGATION & TODAY / ALMANAC RENDERING
═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */
window.navTo = function(name) {
  document.querySelectorAll('.screen').forEach(s => s.classList.remove('active'));
  document.getElementById('s-' + name).classList.add('active');
  document.querySelectorAll('.nav-tab').forEach(b => b.classList.toggle('active', b.dataset.nav === name));

  setTimeout(() => {
    if (name === 'zodiac') {
      if (!threeManagers.element) initElementStage();
      if (state.zodiacView === 'wheel' && !threeManagers.wheel) initWheelStage();
      if (state.zodiacView === 'dialogue' && !threeManagers.alchemy) initAlchemyStage();
    }
    if (name === 'studio' && state.studioTab === 'card' && !threeManagers.card) initCard3D();
    resize3D();
  }, 40);
};

window.openStudioMode = function(mode) {
  navTo('studio');
  switchStudioTab(mode);
};

function renderWeekStrip() {
  const strip = document.getElementById('week-strip');
  const now = new Date();
  const days = ['Su', 'Mo', 'Tu', 'We', 'Th', 'Fr', 'Sa'];
  const monOffset = (now.getDay() + 6) % 7;
  const start = new Date(now);
  start.setDate(now.getDate() - monOffset);
  strip.innerHTML = '';
  for (let i = 0; i < 7; i++) {
    const d = new Date(start);
    d.setDate(start.getDate() + i);
    const isToday = d.toDateString() === now.toDateString();
    strip.innerHTML += `<div class="day-cell ${isToday ? 'today' : ''}"><span class="dw">${days[d.getDay()]}</span><span class="dn tabular">${d.getDate()}</span></div>`;
  }
}

function getDailyPromptIndex(sign, offset = 0) {
  const now = new Date();
  const daySeed = Math.floor(now.getTime() / 86400000);
  return (daySeed + offset) % sign.prompts.length;
}

function renderToday() {
  const strings = UI_STRINGS[state.lang];
  const sign = getSign(state.lensSignId);
  const now = new Date();
  const hr = now.getHours();
  const gIdx = hr < 12 ? 0 : hr < 17 ? 1 : 2;

  document.getElementById('td-greeting').textContent = strings.greetings[gIdx];
  document.getElementById('td-date-line').textContent = new Intl.DateTimeFormat('en-US', {
    timeZone: state.timezone, weekday: 'short', month: 'short', day: 'numeric'
  }).format(now);

  document.getElementById('td-hero-title').textContent = strings.heroTitle;
  document.getElementById('td-hero-sub').textContent = strings.heroSub;
  document.getElementById('btn-write-letter').textContent = strings.writeBtn;
  document.getElementById('btn-make-card').textContent = strings.cardBtn;
  document.getElementById('btn-another-spark').textContent = strings.anotherSpark;
  document.getElementById('td-action-label').textContent = strings.actionLbl;
  document.getElementById('alm-eyebrow').textContent = strings.almanac;

  document.getElementById('nav-lbl-today').textContent = strings.today;
  document.getElementById('nav-lbl-zodiac').textContent = strings.zodiac;
  document.getElementById('nav-lbl-studio').textContent = strings.create;
  document.getElementById('nav-lbl-box').textContent = strings.box;

  // Deterministic Moon Phase at local noon
  const noon = new Date(now.getFullYear(), now.getMonth(), now.getDate(), 12, 0, 0);
  const moon = getMoonPhaseInfo(noon);
  const nextNew = findNextMoonEvent(now, 0);
  const nextFull = findNextMoonEvent(now, 180);

  document.getElementById('td-moon-badge').textContent = `${moon.phaseName} · ${moon.illumination}%`;
  document.getElementById('td-lens-badge').textContent = `${sign.glyph} ${sign.western} · ${sign.element}`;
  document.getElementById('td-spark-lens').textContent = `${state.lensSource} · ${sign.western} (${sign.rashi})`;

  const pIdx = getDailyPromptIndex(sign, state.sparkOffsetToday);
  document.getElementById('td-spark-text').textContent = sign.prompts[pIdx];
  document.getElementById('td-action-text').textContent = sign.action;

  // Show Continue draft if draft text exists
  const draft = localStorage.getItem('unsent_draft_text');
  document.getElementById('continue-draft-wrap').classList.toggle('hidden', !(draft && draft.trim().length > 0));

  // Update fact packet
  state.lastFactPacket = createFactPacket({
    calculationKind: 'daily_sky_and_lens',
    status: state.lensSource === 'Chosen lens' ? 'manually_selected' : 'calculated',
    body: 'Moon & Sun',
    zodiacSystem: 'Tropical + Lahiri reference',
    ayanamsaDegrees: +getLahiriAyanamsa(noon).toFixed(4),
    utcInstant: noon.toISOString(),
    viewerTimezone: state.timezone,
    moonPhase: moon,
    activeLensSignId: sign.id,
    nextNewMoonUTC: nextNew?.toISOString() || null,
    nextFullMoonUTC: nextFull?.toISOString() || null
  });
  document.getElementById('fact-packet-json').textContent = JSON.stringify(state.lastFactPacket, null, 2);

  renderAlmanac(now, nextNew, nextFull);
  syncTodayElement3D();
  syncDesktopCompanion();
}

window.cycleTodaySpark = function() {
  state.sparkOffsetToday += 1;
  renderToday();
};

window.writeFromSpark = function() {
  const sign = getSign(state.lensSignId);
  const prompt = sign.prompts[getDailyPromptIndex(sign, state.sparkOffsetToday)];
  openStudioMode('letter');
  document.getElementById('letter-prompt-line').textContent = prompt;
};

window.continueLastDraft = function() {
  openStudioMode('letter');
};

/* Dreamer's Almanac Calendar */
function renderAlmanac(now, nextNew, nextFull) {
  const fmt = new Intl.DateTimeFormat('en-US', { timeZone: state.timezone, month: 'short', day: 'numeric' });
  const newStr = nextNew ? fmt.format(nextNew) : '—';
  const fullStr = nextFull ? fmt.format(nextFull) : '—';
  document.getElementById('alm-month-title').textContent = new Intl.DateTimeFormat('en-US', { month: 'long', year: 'numeric' }).format(now);
  document.getElementById('alm-upcoming-summary').textContent = `Next New Moon: ${newStr} · Next Full Moon: ${fullStr} (${state.timezone.split('/')[1] || state.timezone})`;

  const y = now.getFullYear(), m = now.getMonth();
  const daysInMonth = new Date(y, m + 1, 0).getDate();
  const firstWd = (new Date(y, m, 1).getDay() + 6) % 7; // Mon=0
  if (!state.selectedAlmanacDate) state.selectedAlmanacDate = `${y}-${String(m + 1).padStart(2, '0')}-${String(now.getDate()).padStart(2, '0')}`;

  const grid = document.getElementById('almanac-grid');
  grid.innerHTML = '';
  for (let b = 0; b < firstWd; b++) grid.innerHTML += `<div></div>`;

  for (let day = 1; day <= daysInMonth; day++) {
    const key = `${y}-${String(m + 1).padStart(2, '0')}-${String(day).padStart(2, '0')}`;
    const dObj = new Date(y, m, day, 12, 0, 0);
    const ph = getMoonPhaseInfo(dObj);
    let icon = '';
    if (ph.phaseName === 'New Moon') icon = '●';
    else if (ph.phaseName === 'Full Moon') icon = '○';
    else if (ph.phaseName === 'First Quarter') icon = '◐';
    else if (ph.phaseName === 'Last Quarter') icon = '◑';

    const isToday = day === now.getDate();
    const isSel = key === state.selectedAlmanacDate;
    const hasNote = state.checkins.some(c => c.dateKey === key);

    const btn = document.createElement('button');
    btn.className = `alm-day tabular ${isToday ? 'today-ring' : ''} ${isSel ? 'selected' : ''}`;
    btn.onclick = () => { state.selectedAlmanacDate = key; renderAlmanac(now, nextNew, nextFull); };
    btn.innerHTML = `<span>${day}</span><span style="font-size:0.65rem;color:${hasNote ? 'var(--rose)' : 'var(--candle)'};">${icon || (hasNote ? '•' : '')}</span>`;
    grid.appendChild(btn);
  }

  const [sy, sm, sd] = state.selectedAlmanacDate.split('-').map(Number);
  const selObj = new Date(sy, sm - 1, sd, 12, 0, 0);
  const selMoon = getMoonPhaseInfo(selObj);
  const linked = state.checkins.filter(c => c.dateKey === state.selectedAlmanacDate);

  document.getElementById('alm-sel-date').textContent = selObj.toLocaleDateString('en-US', { weekday: 'long', month: 'long', day: 'numeric' });
  document.getElementById('alm-sel-sky').textContent = `Sky fact (12:00): ${selMoon.phaseName} · ${selMoon.illumination}% illuminated`;
  document.getElementById('alm-sel-invite').textContent = linked.length
    ? `Your saved check-in: "${linked[0].mood}${linked[0].note ? ' — ' + linked[0].note : ''}"`
    : 'Editorial invitation: What deserves quiet attention tonight before sleep?';
}

window.linkNoteToAlmanacDate = function() {
  openSheet('sh-checkin');
};
