window.switchZodiacView = function(view, btn) {
  state.zodiacView = view;
  btn.parentElement.querySelectorAll('.seg-btn').forEach(b => b.classList.remove('active'));
  btn.classList.add('active');

  ['elements', 'wheel', 'gallery', 'dialogue'].forEach(v => {
    document.getElementById('zv-' + v).classList.toggle('hidden', v !== view);
  });

  document.getElementById('sign-detail-panel').classList.toggle('hidden', view === 'dialogue' || view === 'elements');

  setTimeout(() => {
    if (view === 'elements' && !threeManagers.element) initElementStage();
    if (view === 'wheel' && !threeManagers.wheel) initWheelStage();
    if (view === 'dialogue' && !threeManagers.alchemy) initAlchemyStage();
    resize3D();
  }, 40);
};

window.selectElement3D = function(elemName, btn) {
  state.activeElement3D = elemName;
  document.querySelectorAll('.elem-tab').forEach(b => b.classList.toggle('active', b.dataset.el === elemName));
  renderElementCard();
  syncElementStage3D();
};

function renderElementCard() {
  const el = ELEMENTS_DATA[state.activeElement3D];
  document.getElementById('el-3d-badge').textContent = `${state.activeElement3D} · Interactive 3D`;
  document.getElementById('el-3d-signs').textContent = el.signs.join(' · ');
  document.getElementById('el-card-name').textContent = state.activeElement3D;
  document.getElementById('el-card-themes').textContent = el.themes;
  document.getElementById('el-card-reflection').textContent = el.reflection;

  const chips = document.getElementById('el-sign-chips');
  chips.innerHTML = '';
  SIGNS.filter(s => s.element === state.activeElement3D).forEach(s => {
    const b = document.createElement('button');
    b.className = 'chip-btn';
    b.textContent = `${s.glyph} ${s.western} (${s.rashi})`;
    b.onclick = () => {
      state.selectedZodiacSign = s.id;
      setChosenLens(s.id);
      switchZodiacView('wheel', document.querySelector('[data-zview="wheel"]'));
    };
    chips.appendChild(b);
  });
}

function renderSignGallery() {
  const q = (document.getElementById('sign-search').value || '').toLowerCase().trim();
  const grid = document.getElementById('sign-grid');
  grid.innerHTML = '';
  SIGNS.filter(s =>
    !q || s.western.toLowerCase().includes(q) || s.rashi.toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
    s.element.toLowerCase().includes(q) || s.ruler.toLowerCase().includes(q)
  ).forEach(s => {
    const card = document.createElement('button');
    card.className = `sign-card ${s.id === state.selectedZodiacSign ? 'selected' : ''}`;
    card.onclick = () => selectZodiacSign(s.id);
    card.innerHTML = `
      <span style="font-size:1.45rem;color:${s.color};">${s.glyph}</span>
      <div style="font-weight:600;font-size:0.86rem;">${s.western}</div>
      <div class="label-meta" style="font-size:0.72rem;">${s.rashi} · <span style="font-family:var(--ff-bn)">${s.bn}</span></div>
    `;
    grid.appendChild(card);
  });
}
window.renderSignGallery = renderSignGallery;

  });
}

function selectZodiacSign(id) {
  state.selectedZodiacSign = id;

  document.getElementById('wh-sign-title').textContent = `${s.western} · ${s.rashi}`;
  document.getElementById('wh-sign-meta').textContent = `${s.element} · ${s.modality} · Ruler: ${s.ruler}`;
  document.getElementById('sd-meta').textContent = `${s.element} · ${s.modality} · Traditional Vedic ruler: ${s.ruler}`;
  document.getElementById('sd-name').textContent = `${s.glyph} ${s.western} · ${s.rashi} (${s.hi})`;
  document.getElementById('sd-art').textContent = `Art motif: ${s.art}`;
  document.getElementById('sd-spark').textContent = s.prompts[getDailyPromptIndex(s, state.sparkOffsetDetail)];
  document.getElementById('sd-action').textContent = s.action;

  renderWheelPills();
  renderSignGallery();
  syncWheelTarget();
}

/* Choose My Lens: Deterministic Sun & Lahiri Moon + Birth Time Ambiguity */
window.toggleUnknownTime = function() {
  const checked = document.getElementById('chk-unknown-time').checked;
  document.getElementById('birth-time-in').disabled = checked;
};

window.calculateBirthLens = function() {
  const dateVal = document.getElementById('birth-date-in').value;
  if (!dateVal) { showToast('Enter a birth date first'); return; }
  const unknown = document.getElementById('chk-unknown-time').checked;
  const timeVal = document.getElementById('birth-time-in').value || '12:00';
  const [lat, lon, tz] = document.getElementById('birth-city-in').value.split(',');

  // Compute UTC offset estimate for selected preset timezone on that date
  const [Y, M, D] = dateVal.split('-').map(Number);
  const sampleDate = new Date(Date.UTC(Y, M - 1, D, 12, 0, 0));

  function getOffsetMinutes(dt, zone) {
    const s1 = new Date(dt.toLocaleString('en-US', { timeZone: 'UTC' }));
    const s2 = new Date(dt.toLocaleString('en-US', { timeZone: zone }));
    return Math.round((s2 - s1) / 60000);
  }

  const offMin = getOffsetMinutes(sampleDate, tz);
  const resBox = document.getElementById('lens-result-box');
  resBox.classList.remove('hidden');

  if (unknown) {
    // Sample across the 24h local civil day to detect sign ingress ambiguity!
    const sunSet = new Set();
    const moonSet = new Set();
    for (let h = 0; h <= 24; h += 1) {
      const utcMs = Date.UTC(Y, M - 1, D, h, 0, 0) - offMin * 60000;
      const d = new Date(utcMs);
      sunSet.add(signFromLongitude(getSolarTropicalLongitude(d)).id);
      const lahiriMoonDeg = mod360(getLunarLongitude(d) - getLahiriAyanamsa(d));
      moonSet.add(signFromLongitude(lahiriMoonDeg).id);
    }

    const sunIds = [...sunSet];
    const moonIds = [...moonSet];
    const sunLabel = sunIds.length === 1
      ? `Birth Sun · ${getSign(sunIds[0]).western} (stable across day)`
      : `Birth Sun · ${getSign(sunIds[0]).western} or ${getSign(sunIds[1]).western} (ingress on this date — time matters)`;
    const moonLabel = moonIds.length === 1
      ? `Birth Moon (Lahiri) · ${getSign(moonIds[0]).rashi} (${getSign(moonIds[0]).western})`
      : `Birth Moon (Lahiri) · ${getSign(moonIds[0]).rashi} or ${getSign(moonIds[1]).rashi} (crosses sign on this date — awaiting birth time)`;

    state.lastFactPacket = createFactPacket({
      calculationKind: 'birth_date_only_interval',
      status: (sunIds.length > 1 || moonIds.length > 1) ? 'ambiguous' : 'calculated',
      timezone: tz,
      sunCandidates: sunIds,
      vedicMoonLahiriCandidates: moonIds
    });
    document.getElementById('fact-packet-json').textContent = JSON.stringify(state.lastFactPacket, null, 2);

    resBox.innerHTML = `
      <div class="label-ui" style="color:var(--text-primary);margin-bottom:4px;">${sunLabel}</div>
      <div class="label-ui" style="color:var(--candle);margin-bottom:8px;">${moonLabel}</div>
      <div style="display:flex;gap:8px;flex-wrap:wrap;">
        <button class="chip-btn" onclick="applyCalculatedLens('${sunIds[0]}', 'Birth Sun')">Use Sun lens</button>
        ${moonIds.length === 1 ? `<button class="chip-btn" onclick="applyCalculatedLens('${moonIds[0]}', 'Birth Moon (Lahiri)')">Use Moon Rashi lens</button>` : ''}
      </div>
    `;
  } else {
    const [hh, mm] = timeVal.split(':').map(Number);
    const utcMs = Date.UTC(Y, M - 1, D, hh, mm, 0) - offMin * 60000;
    const instant = new Date(utcMs);
    const sunDeg = getSolarTropicalLongitude(instant);
    const ayanamsa = getLahiriAyanamsa(instant);
    const moonSidDeg = mod360(getLunarLongitude(instant) - ayanamsa);
    const sunSign = signFromLongitude(sunDeg);
    const moonSign = signFromLongitude(moonSidDeg);

    state.lastFactPacket = createFactPacket({
      calculationKind: 'birth_instant_dual_system',
      status: 'calculated',
      utcInstant: instant.toISOString(),
      timezone: tz,
      tropicalSun: { signId: sunSign.id, deg: +sunDeg.toFixed(2) },
      vedicMoonLahiri: { signId: moonSign.id, deg: +moonSidDeg.toFixed(2), ayanamsa: +ayanamsa.toFixed(4) }
    });
    document.getElementById('fact-packet-json').textContent = JSON.stringify(state.lastFactPacket, null, 2);

    resBox.innerHTML = `
      <div class="label-ui" style="color:var(--text-primary);">Birth Sun (Tropical) · ${sunSign.western} (${(sunDeg % 30).toFixed(1)}°)</div>
      <div class="label-ui" style="color:var(--candle);margin:4px 0 8px;">Birth Moon (Lahiri) · ${moonSign.rashi} / ${moonSign.western} (${(moonSidDeg % 30).toFixed(1)}°)</div>
      <div style="display:flex;gap:8px;flex-wrap:wrap;">
        <button class="chip-btn" onclick="applyCalculatedLens('${sunSign.id}', 'Birth Sun')">Use Birth Sun</button>
        <button class="chip-btn" onclick="applyCalculatedLens('${moonSign.id}', 'Birth Moon (Lahiri)')">Use Birth Moon</button>
      </div>
    `;
  }
};

window.applyCalculatedLens = function(id, sourceLabel) {
  state.lensSignId = id;
  state.lensSource = sourceLabel;
  state.selectedZodiacSign = id;
  persistState();
  renderToday();
  selectZodiacSign(id);
  closeSheets();
  showToast(`${sourceLabel} · ${getSign(id).western}`);
};

/* Two-Sign Conversation Cards (No Percentages, No Verdicts) */
window.setDialogueCtx = function(ctx, btn) {
  state.dialogueCtx = ctx;
  btn.parentElement.querySelectorAll('.seg-btn').forEach(b => b.classList.remove('active'));
  btn.classList.add('active');
  renderDialogue();
};

function renderDialogue() {
  const a = getSign(state.pairA), b = getSign(state.pairB);
  document.getElementById('pair-a-lbl').textContent = `Sign A: ${a.western}`;
  document.getElementById('pair-b-lbl').textContent = `Sign B: ${b.western}`;
  document.getElementById('alc-badge').textContent = `${a.element} × ${b.element}`;

  document.getElementById('dlg-q1-who').textContent = `Question for ${a.western} (${a.rashi})`;
  document.getElementById('dlg-q2-who').textContent = `Question for ${b.western} (${b.rashi})`;

  const ctxMap = {
    friendship: {
      q1: `What small ordinary kindness from ${b.western} makes conversation feel effortless?`,
      q2: `What quiet space do you wish ${a.western} always knew was open for them?`,
      shared: a.id === 'gemini' && b.id === 'pisces'
        ? 'Describe the same memory twice: once as a sharp headline, and once as a color.'
        : `Share one memory in two lines each — one from ${a.element.toLowerCase()} sensation, one from ${b.element.toLowerCase()} feeling.`
    },
    family: {
      q1: `What unspoken rule at home do you notice differently than ${b.western}?`,
      q2: `What ordinary moment with ${a.western} would you keep in a tin box?`,
      shared: 'Name one family habit worth keeping and one rule you both want to soften gently.'
    },
    creative: {
      q1: `Where does your ${a.element.toLowerCase()} instinct want to start faster or slower than ${b.western}?`,
      q2: `What detail in ${a.western}'s sketch sparks your next line?`,
      shared: 'Take 2 minutes to make one tiny sketch or four lines together without editing.'
    }
  }[state.dialogueCtx];

  document.getElementById('dlg-q1-text').textContent = ctxMap.q1;
  document.getElementById('dlg-q2-text').textContent = ctxMap.q2;
  document.getElementById('dlg-shared-text').textContent = ctxMap.shared;
  syncAlchemy3D();
}

function buildPairPickers() {
  const fill = (elId, key) => {
    const box = document.getElementById(elId);
    box.innerHTML = '';
    SIGNS.forEach(s => {
      const btn = document.createElement('button');
      btn.className = 'sign-card';
      btn.onclick = () => { state[key] = s.id; closeSheets(); renderDialogue(); };
      btn.innerHTML = `<span style="font-size:1.3rem;color:${s.color};">${s.glyph}</span><span style="font-size:0.8rem;font-weight:600;">${s.western}</span>`;
      box.appendChild(btn);
    });
  };
  fill('pair-grid-a', 'pairA');
  fill('pair-grid-b', 'pairB');
}

/* ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   7. STUDIO: WRITE & SEAL LETTERS + 9:16 CARDS
═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */
window.switchStudioTab = function(tab) {
  state.studioTab = tab;
  document.getElementById('tab-st-letter').classList.toggle('active', tab === 'letter');
  document.getElementById('tab-st-card').classList.toggle('active', tab === 'card');
  document.getElementById('st-letter-pane').classList.toggle('hidden', tab !== 'letter');
  document.getElementById('st-card-pane').classList.toggle('hidden', tab !== 'card');
  if (tab === 'card') {
    if (!threeManagers.card) initCard3D();
    setTimeout(() => { resize3D(); syncCardPreview(); }, 40);
  }
};

function renderStudioControls() {
  const recRow = document.getElementById('recipient-row');
  recRow.innerHTML = '';
  RECIPIENTS.forEach(rec => {
    const b = document.createElement('button');
    b.className = `seg-btn ${rec.id === state.recipientId ? 'active' : ''}`;
    b.textContent = rec.label;
    b.onclick = () => {
      state.recipientId = rec.id;
      state.recipientPromptIdx = 0;
      document.getElementById('letter-salutation').value = rec.salutation;
      renderStudioControls();
    };
    recRow.appendChild(b);
  });

  const activeRec = RECIPIENTS.find(r => r.id === state.recipientId) || RECIPIENTS[3];
  document.getElementById('letter-prompt-line').textContent = activeRec.prompts[state.recipientPromptIdx % activeRec.prompts.length];

  const paperRow = document.getElementById('paper-row');
  paperRow.innerHTML = '';
  PAPERS.forEach(p => {
    const b = document.createElement('button');
    b.className = `seg-btn ${p.id === state.paperId ? 'active' : ''}`;
    b.textContent = p.name;
    b.onclick = () => {
      state.paperId = p.id;
      const surface = document.getElementById('letter-paper');
      PAPERS.forEach(x => surface.classList.remove(x.cls));
      surface.classList.add(p.cls);
      renderStudioControls();
    };
    paperRow.appendChild(b);
  });
}

window.cycleRecipientPrompt = function() {
  state.recipientPromptIdx += 1;
  renderStudioControls();
};

window.onLetterInput = function() {
  const txt = document.getElementById('letter-body').value;
  const words = txt.trim() ? txt.trim().split(/\s+/).length : 0;
  document.getElementById('letter-word-count').textContent = `${words} ${words === 1 ? 'word' : 'words'}`;
  try {
    localStorage.setItem('unsent_draft_text', txt);
    localStorage.setItem('unsent_draft_salutation', document.getElementById('letter-salutation').value);
    document.getElementById('letter-save-status').textContent = 'Autosaved locally';
  } catch {
    document.getElementById('letter-save-status').textContent = 'Editor active (storage full)';
  }
};

function restoreDraft() {
  const txt = localStorage.getItem('unsent_draft_text');
  const sal = localStorage.getItem('unsent_draft_salutation');
  if (sal) document.getElementById('letter-salutation').value = sal;
  if (txt) {
    document.getElementById('letter-body').value = txt;
    onLetterInput();
  }
}

window.runLocalReflection = function(mode) {
  const txt = document.getElementById('letter-body').value.trim();
  if (!txt) { showToast('Write a few lines first'); return; }

  // Safe local distress keyword awareness (supports English & transliterated indicators)
  if (/\b(suicide|kill myself|end my life|self[- ]?harm|don't want to live)\b/i.test(txt)) {
    openSheet('sh-support');
    return;
  }

  const box = document.getElementById('local-assist-box');
  box.classList.remove('hidden');
  const snippet = txt.split(/[.!?\n]/).filter(Boolean)[0]?.trim() || txt.slice(0, 80);
  if (mode === 'reflect') {
    document.getElementById('local-assist-title').textContent = 'Quiet reflection (on-device)';
    document.getElementById('local-assist-text').textContent = `It sounds like there is tenderness and unspoken weight around "${snippet.slice(0, 72)}". You do not have to resolve everything tonight; giving these words paper is already enough.`;
  } else {
    document.getElementById('local-assist-title').textContent = 'Four editable lines (from your words)';
    const words = txt.split(/\s+/).slice(0, 24);
    const l1 = words.slice(0, 6).join(' ');
    const l2 = words.slice(6, 12).join(' ') || 'quiet ink on tonight’s page';
    const l3 = words.slice(12, 18).join(' ') || 'never sent, still completely true';
    const l4 = words.slice(18, 24).join(' ') || 'held gently under tonight’s sky.';
    document.getElementById('local-assist-text').innerText = `${l1}\n${l2}\n${l3}\n${l4}`;
  }
};

window.sealAndKeepLetter = function() {
  const body = document.getElementById('letter-body').value.trim();
  if (!body) { showToast('Write a line before sealing'); return; }

  const stamp = document.getElementById('letter-stamp');
  stamp.classList.add('stamped');

  const item = {
    id: 'ltr_' + Date.now(),
    type: 'letter',
    recipient: RECIPIENTS.find(r => r.id === state.recipientId)?.label || 'Custom',
    salutation: document.getElementById('letter-salutation').value,
    body,
    paperId: state.paperId,
    stampedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
    favorite: false
  };
  state.items.unshift(item);
  localStorage.removeItem('unsent_draft_text');
  persistState();
  renderBox();
  renderToday();
  showToast('Sealed · NEVER SENT · Saved to My Box');
};

window.turnLetterIntoCard = function() {
  const body = document.getElementById('letter-body').value.trim();
  if (body) document.getElementById('card-text-in').value = body.slice(0, 220);
  switchStudioTab('card');
};

/* Release Ritual */
window.setReleaseMode = function(mode, btn) {
  state.releaseMode = mode;
  btn.parentElement.querySelectorAll('.seg-btn').forEach(b => b.classList.remove('active'));
  btn.classList.add('active');
  rebuildReleaseParticles();
};

window.confirmReleaseLetter = function() {
  document.getElementById('letter-body').value = '';
  document.getElementById('letter-stamp').classList.remove('stamped');
  localStorage.removeItem('unsent_draft_text');
  onLetterInput();
  renderToday();
  closeSheets();
  showToast(`Released with ${state.releaseMode}`);
};

/* Dictation */
window.startVoiceDictation = function() {
  const SR = window.SpeechRecognition || window.webkitSpeechRecognition;
  if (!SR) {
    closeSheets();
    showToast('Speech dictation unavailable in this browser — typing ready');
    return;
  }
  closeSheets();
  const rec = new SR();
  rec.lang = state.lang === 'bn' ? 'bn-IN' : state.lang === 'hi' ? 'hi-IN' : 'en-IN';
  rec.onresult = (e) => {
    const tr = e.results[0][0].transcript;
    const area = document.getElementById('letter-body');
    area.value = (area.value ? area.value + ' ' : '') + tr;
    onLetterInput();
  };
  rec.onerror = () => showToast('Microphone permission denied — typing stays available');
  rec.start();
};

/* 9:16 Card Studio */
const CARD_STYLES = {
  obsidian: { bg: '#0E0B1A', fg: '#F5F2EC' },
  parchment: { bg: '#F5EAD6', fg: '#1D1812' },
  indigo: { bg: '#1B1740', fg: '#F4F1FA' },
  rose: { bg: '#2B1924', fg: '#F8EAEF' }
};

window.setCardTheme = function(t, btn) {
  state.cardTheme = t;
  btn.parentElement.querySelectorAll('.seg-btn').forEach(b => b.classList.remove('active'));
  btn.classList.add('active');
  syncCardPreview();
};

function syncCardPreview() {
  const sign = getSign(state.lensSignId);
  const st = CARD_STYLES[state.cardTheme];
  const frame = document.getElementById('story-preview');
  const content = document.getElementById('story-content');

  frame.style.background = st.bg;
  frame.style.color = st.fg;
  content.classList.toggle('safe-crop', document.getElementById('chk-safe-crop').checked);

  const mood = document.getElementById('card-mood-in').value.trim();
  const txt = document.getElementById('card-text-in').value.trim() || sign.prompts[getDailyPromptIndex(sign, state.sparkOffsetToday)];
  const fz = document.getElementById('card-font-size').value;

  document.getElementById('card-top-label').textContent = mood ? `${mood} · Sky Card` : 'Sky Card';
  document.getElementById('card-title-out').textContent = `${sign.glyph} ${sign.western} · ${sign.rashi}`;
  const bodyEl = document.getElementById('card-body-out');
  bodyEl.textContent = txt;
  bodyEl.style.fontSize = fz;

  const showMoon = document.getElementById('chk-show-moon').checked;
  const moon = getMoonPhaseInfo(new Date());
  document.getElementById('card-moon-out').textContent = showMoon ? `${moon.phaseName} · ${moon.illumination}%` : '';
  document.getElementById('card-wm-out').textContent = document.getElementById('chk-watermark').checked ? 'UNSENT' : '';

  syncCard3DElement();
}
window.syncCardPreview = syncCardPreview;

window.exportCardPNG = async function() {
  const W = 1080, H = 1920;
  const canvas = document.createElement('canvas');
  canvas.width = W; canvas.height = H;
  const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');

  const st = CARD_STYLES[state.cardTheme];
  const sign = getSign(state.lensSignId);
  ctx.fillStyle = st.bg;
  ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H);

  if (threeManagers.card) {
    threeManagers.card.renderer.render(threeManagers.card.scene, threeManagers.card.camera);
    ctx.drawImage(document.getElementById('canvas-card-3d'), 0, 0, W, H);
  }

  ctx.fillStyle = st.fg;
  ctx.textAlign = 'center';
  const safe = document.getElementById('chk-safe-crop').checked ? 220 : 140;

  ctx.globalAlpha = 0.72;
  ctx.font = '500 32px Inter, sans-serif';
  ctx.fillText(document.getElementById('card-top-label').textContent, W / 2, safe);

  ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
  ctx.font = '600 66px "Plus Jakarta Sans", Inter, sans-serif';
  ctx.fillText(`${sign.western} · ${sign.rashi}`, W / 2, 1080);

  const txt = document.getElementById('card-body-out').textContent;
  ctx.font = '400 44px Newsreader, "Noto Sans Devanagari", "Hind Siliguri", serif';
  const words = txt.split(/\s+/);
  let line = '', y = 1190;
  for (const w of words) {
    const test = line + w + ' ';
    if (ctx.measureText(test).width > 820 && line) {
      ctx.fillText(line.trim(), W / 2, y);
      line = w + ' ';
      y += 64;
    } else { line = test; }
  }
  if (line.trim()) ctx.fillText(line.trim(), W / 2, y);

  ctx.globalAlpha = 0.65;
  ctx.font = '500 30px Inter, sans-serif';
  const moonLine = document.getElementById('card-moon-out').textContent;
  if (moonLine) ctx.fillText(moonLine, W / 2, H - safe - 55);
  if (document.getElementById('chk-watermark').checked) ctx.fillText('UNSENT', W / 2, H - safe);

  // Use Web Share API if file sharing supported, else clean PNG download
  canvas.toBlob(async (blob) => {
    if (!blob) return;
    const file = new File([blob], `unsent-${sign.id}-card.png`, { type: 'image/png' });
    if (navigator.canShare && navigator.canShare({ files: [file] })) {
      try {
        await navigator.share({ files: [file], title: 'UNSENT Sky Card' });
        showToast('Share sheet opened');
        return;
      } catch {}
    }
    const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
    const a = document.createElement('a');
    a.href = url;
    a.download = `unsent-${sign.id}-1080x1920.png`;
    a.click();
    URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
    showToast('1080×1920 PNG saved');
  }, 'image/png');
};

window.saveCardToBox = function() {
  const sign = getSign(state.lensSignId);
  const txt = document.getElementById('card-text-in').value.trim() || document.getElementById('card-body-out').textContent;
  state.items.unshift({
    id: 'crd_' + Date.now(),
    type: 'card',
    sign: sign.western,
    mood: document.getElementById('card-mood-in').value.trim() || 'Quiet',
    body: txt,
    stampedAt: new Date().toISOString()
  });
  persistState();
  renderBox();
  showToast('Card saved to My Box');
};

window.toggleVaultLock = function() {
  state.vaultLocked = !state.vaultLocked;
  document.getElementById('vault-locked-view').classList.toggle('hidden', !state.vaultLocked);
  document.getElementById('vault-open-view').classList.toggle('hidden', state.vaultLocked);

window.setBoxFilter = function(f, btn) {
  state.boxFilter = f;
  btn.parentElement.querySelectorAll('.seg-btn').forEach(b => b.classList.remove('active'));
  btn.classList.add('active');
  renderBox();
};

function renderBox() {
  document.getElementById('demo-samples-card').classList.toggle('hidden', state.hideSampleCard);
  const list = document.getElementById('box-list');
  const q = (document.getElementById('box-search').value || '').toLowerCase().trim();

  if (state.boxFilter === 'recap') {
    const totalLetters = state.items.filter(i => i.type === 'letter').length;
    const totalCards = state.items.filter(i => i.type === 'card').length;
    const moodCounts = {};
    state.checkins.forEach(c => { moodCounts[c.mood] = (moodCounts[c.mood] || 0) + 1; });
    const moodLines = Object.entries(moodCounts).map(([m, c]) => `${m}: ${c}`).join(' · ') || 'No check-ins recorded yet';
    list.innerHTML = `
      <div class="card">
        <div class="label-meta">My year in feelings (real entries only)</div>
        <h3 class="h-display" style="font-size:1.2rem;margin:6px 0 10px;">${totalLetters} letters · ${totalCards} cards</h3>
        <p class="h-sub" style="font-size:0.88rem;">Emotional weather check-ins: ${moodLines}</p>
      </div>
    `;
    return;
  }

  const filtered = state.items.filter(item => {
    if (state.boxFilter !== 'all' && item.type !== state.boxFilter) return false;
    if (!q) return true;
    return (item.body || '').toLowerCase().includes(q) || (item.recipient || '').toLowerCase().includes(q);
  });

  if (!filtered.length) {
    list.innerHTML = `<div class="card" style="text-align:center;"><p class="h-sub">Your box is quiet here. Write a letter or save a card whenever you want.</p></div>`;
    return;
  }

  list.innerHTML = filtered.map(item => `
    <div class="list-item">
      <div style="display:flex;justify-content:space-between;align-items:center;margin-bottom:6px;">
        <span class="label-meta">${item.type === 'letter' ? `To ${item.recipient} · NEVER SENT` : `Feeling Card · ${item.mood || 'Quiet'}`}</span>
        <span class="label-meta">${new Date(item.stampedAt).toLocaleDateString()}</span>
      </div>
      <p style="font-size:0.92rem;line-height:1.55;margin-bottom:10px;">${escapeHtml(item.body.slice(0, 180))}${item.body.length > 180 ? '…' : ''}</p>
      <div style="display:flex;gap:8px;">
        <button class="chip-btn" style="min-height:30px;padding:3px 10px;font-size:0.75rem;" onclick="duplicateItem('${item.id}')">Duplicate draft</button>
        <button class="chip-btn" style="min-height:30px;padding:3px 10px;font-size:0.75rem;color:var(--rose);" onclick="deleteItem('${item.id}')">Delete</button>
      </div>
    </div>
  `).join('');
}
window.renderBox = renderBox;

window.duplicateItem = function(id) {
  const item = state.items.find(i => i.id === id);
  if (!item) return;
  openStudioMode('letter');
  document.getElementById('letter-body').value = item.body;
  onLetterInput();
};

window.deleteItem = function(id) {
  state.items = state.items.filter(i => i.id !== id);
  persistState();
  renderBox();
  showToast('Removed from local box');
};

window.removeSampleEntries = function() {
  state.hideSampleCard = true;
  persistState();
  renderBox();
};

/* Mood Check-in */
let pickedMood = 'Quiet';
function buildMoodChips() {
  const moods = ['Quiet', 'Heavy', 'Hopeful', 'Tender', 'Restless', 'Grateful'];
  const box = document.getElementById('mood-chips');
  box.innerHTML = '';
  moods.forEach(m => {
    const b = document.createElement('button');
    b.className = `seg-btn ${m === pickedMood ? 'active' : ''}`;
    b.textContent = m;
    b.onclick = () => { pickedMood = m; buildMoodChips(); };
    box.appendChild(b);
  });
}

window.saveCheckIn = function() {
  const note = document.getElementById('checkin-note').value.trim();
  const key = state.selectedAlmanacDate || new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10);
  state.checkins.unshift({ dateKey: key, mood: pickedMood, note, at: Date.now() });
  document.getElementById('checkin-note').value = '';
  persistState();
  closeSheets();
  renderToday();
  renderBox();
  showToast('Emotional weather saved');
};

window.openSheet = function(id) {
  closeSheets(false);
  document.getElementById('backdrop').classList.add('open');
  document.getElementById(id).classList.add('open');
  if (id === 'sh-release') {
    setTimeout(() => {
      if (!threeManagers.release) initRelease3D();
      resize3D();
    }, 50);
  }
};

window.closeSheets = function(hideBg = true) {
  if (hideBg) document.getElementById('backdrop').classList.remove('open');
  document.querySelectorAll('.sheet').forEach(s => s.classList.remove('open'));
};

function escapeHtml(str) {
  return str.replace(/[&<>"']/g, m => ({ '&':'&amp;', '<':'&lt;', '>':'&gt;', '"':'&quot;', "'":'&#39;' }[m]));
}

function showToast(msg) {
  const t = document.getElementById('toast');
  t.textContent = msg;
  t.classList.add('show');
  setTimeout(() => t.classList.remove('show'), 2200);
}

5. sw.js
JavaScript
const CACHE_NAME = 'unsent-astral-v1';
const PUBLIC_ASSETS = [
  './',
  './index.html',
  './manifest.webmanifest',
  './assets/icon.svg',
  './styles/app.css',
  './src/app.js',
  './src/data/zodiac.js',
  './src/engine/astronomy.js',
  './src/engine/storage.js',
  './src/three/elements3d.js'
];

self.addEventListener('install', (event) => {
  event.waitUntil(
    caches.open(CACHE_NAME).then((cache) => cache.addAll(PUBLIC_ASSETS))
  );
  self.skipWaiting();
});

self.addEventListener('activate', (event) => {
  event.waitUntil(
    caches.keys().then((keys) =>
      Promise.all(keys.filter((k) => k !== CACHE_NAME).map((k) => caches.delete(k)))
    )
  );
  self.clients.claim();
});

self.addEventListener('fetch', (event) => {
  if (event.request.method !== 'GET') return;
  const url = new URL(event.request.url);
  if (url.origin !== self.location.origin) return;
  event.respondWith(
    caches.match(event.request).then((cached) => cached || fetch(event.request))
  );
});
